COHERENCE FRAMEWORK
Schema v11

SECTION ONE -- METHOD
[SEC:BASIC_QUALITIES_OF_COHERENCE]
==========================================================================
BASIC QUALITIES OF COHERENCE

------------------------------
Nine qualities. Mutually dependent.

INTERNAL CYCLE PROPERTIES [Q1-Q4]

  1. GENERATIVE CYCLE
     Completes a loop: takes in, transforms, returns.
     Failure mode: cycle collapse or performance without function.

  2. SURPLUS PRODUCTION
     Outputs exceed cost of running the cycle across an honest window.
     Moral content lives here.
     Failure mode: consumption dominance.

  3. CORRECTION CAPACITY
     Detects contradiction between outputs and orientation.
     Moves without external pressure.
     Failure mode: correction absence or capture.

  9. CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
     Outputs traceable to actions within boundary with sufficient
     resolution to ground correction. Scalar -- partial legibility
     produces partial correction capacity.
     Failure mode: correction orphaned. Not captured, not absent --
     unanchored.
     Distinct from Q3 [mechanism present but cannot land] and Q7
     [surface intact but chain broken].
     Correction pathway: causal reconstruction before correction
     attempt. Establish chain first; then correction can anchor.
     Weapon: Engineered Causal Diffusion [scores]. Structural Causal
     Diffusion [failure mode only, does not score].
     Primary proof [weapon]: financial derivatives market pre-2008.
     Primary proof [structural]: supply chain labor conditions.
     Note: placed functionally between Q3 and Q4 in structural order;
     numbered Q9 to preserve case library references.

  4. GENUINE AXIS
     Orientation generated internally with self-examination present.
     Failure mode: imposed axis.

BOUNDARY PROPERTIES [Q5-Q6]

  5. BOUNDARY INTEGRITY
     Honest definition of who is inside the boundary.
     Does not shift to exclude populations experiencing harm.
     Failure mode: boundary manipulation.
     Linked to Q7 -- boundary drift is a measurement integrity failure.

  6. PROPORTIONAL RESPONSE
     Correction force proportional to deviation detected, not to the
     threat correction poses to existing power.
     Failure mode: overcorrection as suppression.

TEMPORAL PROPERTIES [Q7-Q8]

  7. MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
     System maintains honest, accessible surface by which outputs can
     be evaluated against stated orientation. Includes boundary honesty,
     signal sequencing, correction trigger accessibility.
     Failure mode: measurement surface captured, drifted, or destroyed.
     Most load-bearing quality -- Q1-Q6 intact with Q7 destroyed
     misreads as Variable or High while consuming.
     Correction pathway: surface reconstruction, not cycle repair.
     Primary proof: Enron [deployed]. Doctor Manhattan [structural].

  8. TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
     System transmits correctable axis to successors, not locked state.
     Fidelity necessary but insufficient.
     Failure mode: Fertile Lock.
     Q4 = within-window axis integrity. Q8 = what crosses transmission
     boundary.
     Primary proof: Faeanor -> sons [Q4/Q8 distinction clearest case].

  10. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY [v11]
     Successor's capacity to revise transmitted content against new
     evidence without losing transmission fidelity to founding
     generative principle.
     High Q10 = correctable inheritance [revises conclusions, preserves
     method]. Low Q10 = Fertile Lock [cannot revise without losing
     identity].
     Structural order: after Q8 [what crosses boundary], before terminal
     states. Q8 is transmission; Q10 is reception.
     Distinct from Q8: Q8 measures what is sent. Q10 measures successor's
     capacity to work with what was received.
     PRIMARY proof: Benedictine order [high Q10 -- correctable
     transmission] vs Dominican order [low Q10 -- lock into Aquinas].
     Same medieval transmission context, different Q10 outcomes.
     Secondary: Bell Labs -> Lucent -> Nokia [Q10 declining at each
     transmission -- shape preserved, generative content absent].

  11. CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING [Q11]
     Axis traceable to actual outputs not just internal conviction
     or stated orientation. Q4 [genuine axis] asks whether axis was
     internally generated and examined. Q11 asks whether that axis
     is anchored to real-world causal outputs.
     Distinct from Q9 [Causal Legibility]: Q9 anchors correction to
     actor. Q11 anchors axis to reality. Q9 makes correction legible;
     Q11 makes axis legible.
     Failure mode: Sincere Delusion -- genuine Q4 [examined, internal]
     with zero connection to actual outputs. Axis real to the system;
     not real to the world it claims to act on.
     Structural position: after Q4 [axis must exist before it can be
     grounded], before Q5 [boundary integrity requires axis to be
     real not just sincere].
     Primary proof candidates: Rupture [Maiad -- genuine Q4, axis not
     grounded in achievable outputs]; cargo cults [sincere axis,
     no causal connection to desired outcomes].

  12. SUBSTRATE REGENERATION [Q12]
     System's capacity to restore the substrate its cycle depends on.
     Not passive non-consumption -- active regeneration.
     Distinct from Q2: Q2 measures surplus relative to cycle cost.
     Q12 measures substrate health independent of cycle. System can
     have high Q2 [surplus > cost] while degrading substrate
     [agriculture: high yield, soil depletion simultaneously].
     Distinct from Q3: Q3 corrects cycle deviation. Q12 corrects
     substrate deviation. Different target.
     Failure mode: Substrate Atrophy -- Q1 continues running but
     substrate diminishes each cycle. System doesn't notice because
     Q7 measures cycle output not substrate health.
     Structural position: after Q1 [cycle must exist before substrate
     health measurable], before Q2 [substrate health constrains
     surplus ceiling long-term].

     MULTI-SUBSTRATE REQUIREMENT [v11]: coherence requires Q12
     operating across ALL dependent substrates simultaneously.
     Single-substrate coherence = plateau at best, extraction at worst.
     System running Q1 on one substrate while depleting another is
     extracting -- extraction obscured across substrate boundaries.
     Q13 must distribute surplus across substrates not just outward.
     If any substrate is net-negative, system is extracting from it
     regardless of Q2 outputs elsewhere.
     Bell Labs confirmation: intellectual [talent], institutional
     [AT&T surplus], physical [infrastructure] -- all regenerated
     simultaneously. Single substrate failure would have terminated
     the coherence window.
     Industrial agriculture negative proof: Q2 high [food], Q12
     failing [soil depletion, water table], Q13 near-zero [profit
     leaves community]. Three substrates each degrading while Q2
     appears positive.
     Planetary negative proof [Venus]: Q12 failure at maximum scale.
     Lost geological greenhouse gas sequestration -- runaway feedback
     destroyed substrate entirely. Earth's plate tectonics = Q12
     mechanism; Venus lacks it. Strongest planetary Q12 failure case.

     Nature-based climate solutions [Guardian/UN 2026 -- strongest
     real-world confirmation]: mangroves, peatlands, forests = Q12
     mechanisms suppressed by extraction economy. Solutions exist,
     work, require no new technology. $30 spent harming nature for
     every $1 protecting it [UN 2026]. Only 1.5% of climate funding
     to nature-based solutions despite mangroves sequestering at 18x
     forest rate. Ratio [30:1 against] confirms managed coherence
     collapse at ecological substrate -- not ignorance, structural
     priority inversion serving extraction interests.
     Primary proof candidates: mycorrhizal networks [restore soil
     biology]; regenerative agriculture [builds topsoil]; old-growth
     forest [self-regenerating substrate].
     Negative proof: industrial agriculture [Q2 high, Q12 failing];
     overfishing [catch surplus maintained until collapse].

  13. SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION [Q13]
     System's capacity to release surplus outward to adjacent systems.
     Active dispersal not passive non-extraction.
     Tension pair with Q12: Q12 pulls inward [restore substrate];
     Q13 pushes outward [release surplus]. Both required.
     Q12 without Q13: healthy but hoarding. Q13 without Q12:
     generous but substrate-depleting.
     Distinct from Q2: Q2 measures surplus exists. Q13 measures
     whether surplus crosses boundary outward.
     Failure mode: Accumulation Lock -- Q2 high, Q12 adequate,
     Q13 near-zero. System retains surplus rather than releasing it.
     Structural position: pairs with Q12 [after Q1, before Q2
     ceiling is set by both substrate health and dispersal rate].
     Primary proof candidates: mycorrhizal networks [release
     phosphorus outward]; Gift Index cases [CERN, Linux, Bell Labs
     all high Q13]; fruit [releases calories outward].
     Negative proof: corporate cash hoarding [Q2 high, Q12 ok,
     Q13 zero]; monopoly accumulation.

  14. TENSION MAINTENANCE [Q14]
     System's active capacity to sustain productive tension between
     opposing qualities without collapsing to either pole.
     Second-order quality -- holds other qualities in relationship.
     Distinct from Q3 [corrects deviation]: Q14 maintains the
     tension that makes Q3 necessary. Distinct from Q1 [generates]:
     Q14 holds Q1 and Q3 in relationship without one dominating.
     Failure mode: Tension Collapse -- system resolves tension by
     eliminating one pole. Axis-Lock [Q4 eliminates Q3] or
     Sub-Structural [no axis, no tension possible].
     Biological confirmation: homeostasis [competing chemical/thermal
     states sustained]; flow state [challenge/skill balance actively
     maintained]; mycorrhizal exchange [mutualism sustained against
     drift toward parasitism].
     Planetary confirmation: Earth sustains geological tension between
     heat production and sequestration via plate tectonics. Venus lost
     this -- Q14 failure producing Q12 collapse.
     Structural position: meta-quality, operates across all scales
     where tension architecture is present.

  15. TENSION DISSOLUTION [Q15 -- active opposite of Q14]
     System's tendency to resolve productive tension by eliminating
     one pole rather than maintaining the relationship.
     Passive structural drift toward false resolution -- not weapon
     deployment but built-in architecture in some systems.
     Distinct from Q14 failure [Tension Collapse]: Q14 fails through
     inability; Q15 is constitutive drive toward resolution.
     Failure mode: False Harmony -- apparent stability achieved by
     eliminating corrective pole. No friction, no Q3, no deviation
     detectable. Looks coherent, isn't.
     Biological confirmation: cancerous microenvironment [dissolves
     immune tension -- NK cells suppressed]; monoculture [eliminates
     ecological tension]; Venus [dissolved geological tension cycle].
     Social confirmation: consensus cultures, authoritarian structures,
     echo chambers -- all built to dissolve tension as feature.
     Tension pair with Q14: Q14 sustains; Q15 dissolves. System
     with high Q15 and low Q14 = False Harmony trajectory.

STRUCTURAL ORDER [simple to complex -- scale of emergence]

  Generative cycle         precondition [cell scale]
  Boundary integrity       keeps measurement population honest [cell]
  Correction capacity      keeps cycle honest [cell]
  Substrate regeneration   sustains the precondition [Q12 -- ecosystem]
  Surplus distribution     releases surplus outward [Q13 -- organism]
  Surplus production       moral value lives here [multicellular]
  Causal legibility        anchors correction to actor [organism]
  Proportional response    keeps correction from becoming next failure
  Measurement integrity    keeps correction capacity functional
  Genuine axis             gives correction direction [collective/person]
  Causal axis grounding    anchors axis to real outputs [Q11 -- person]
  Transmission selectivity what coherence produces across time [person]
  Successor autonomy       ascending path requires this [person only]
  Tension maintenance      sustains productive tension [Q14 -- meta]
  Tension dissolution      drives false resolution [Q15 -- meta/opposite]

ANTI-COHERENCE REVERSE ORDER

  Fertile Lock -> Measurement destruction -> Boundary manipulation
  -> Axis imposition -> Correction capture -> Causal orphaning
  -> Consumption dominance -> Cycle collapse

  Q6 failure runs parallel, not sequential.

[HDR:VERSION_CHANGE_CONVENTION__V9_]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
VERSION CHANGE CONVENTION [v9]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

VERSION INCREMENT [vN -> vN+1] when:
  - New quality added to stack [Q1-Q9 level change]
  - New section added or sections restructured
  - Category renamed across full document [Structural Coherence rename]
  - New formal category added [Intrinsic, Nascent, Seeded Nascent]
  - New failure state added to measurement procedure
  - New honest limit category added [T2 Permanent]
  - New formal designation added [Evil/Good, Mixed Coherence]
  - New measurement track added [Track A-E]
  - Weapon index restructured or major weapon formally defined

[HDR:MERGE___REMOVE_LOGIC__EXECUTE_]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
MERGE / REMOVE LOGIC [execute at version change]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Run this checklist before finalizing any new version file.

REMOVE:
  - WIP items marked RESOLVED or CLOSED -- delete entirely
  - "deferred to vN" references where N <= current version
  - "Move to Pending" markers -- item should already be there
  - Duplicate section content [same definition in two sections]
  - Stale transcript-pending notes where content now confirmed
  - Orphan separator lines [=== or --- with no adjacent content]

MERGE:
  - Standalone findings that duplicate Theory section content
  - Psychology parallel + Fiction/Real table [same purpose]
  - Pending items that are fully complete -- remove from Pending
  - WIP sub-items that belong under a parent item

COMPRESS:
  - Version convention lists > 10 items -- summarize by category
  - Self-cleaning convention -- fold into version convention
  - Pending list items marked complete in all sub-points -- remove

FORMAT:
  - Max 2 consecutive blank lines
  - No blank line immediately after === separator
  - No double === separators [run dedup pass]
  - No duplicate subsection headers within 10 lines of each other

AUDIT COMMANDS [run before saving]:
  Double separators:  count(===\n===) == 0
  Triple blanks:      count(\n\n\n) == 0
  Stale deferred:     count('deferred to v[N-1 or earlier]') == 0
  Orphan RESOLVED:    count('RESOLVED [v') in Active List == 0

FORMAT RULE: all lines <= 78 chars. Separators exempt.

SECTION AND MERGE LOGIC [applies everywhere]:
  AUTO-MERGE: two entries in same major section addressing same
    structural phenomenon from different angles -> merge on next
    version increment. Flag [MERGE CANDIDATE] if identified mid-session.
    Trigger: same Q-property target + same mechanism = merge.
    Trigger: sequential argument split across adjacent sections = merge.
    Trigger: one section is restatement of adjacent section = compress
    into cross-reference or subsection.

  ADD NEW SECTION: entry has distinct mechanism, distinct Q-property
    target, AND no existing section covers it within 200 lines.
    Otherwise: add as subsection or extend nearest related entry.

  CROSS-REFERENCE instead of merge when: entries are in different
    section types [empirical vs speculative; method vs case library].
    Boundary between sections is load-bearing -- do not collapse.

  COMPRESS: entry where full detail lives elsewhere -> reduce to
    core claim + pointer. Never duplicate substantial content.

  GIFT INDEX specific: Primary / Secondary / Closed / Sacrificial
    subcategories separated by ---------- for reading clarity.
    New entries slot into nearest existing subcategory.

  WEAPON INDEX specific: entries with identical Q-target + mechanism
    -> merge with note. Sole proof of a type -> keep regardless of
    length. Remove only if no primary proof case.

NAME COMPRESSION POLICY:
  Long technical names compressed on second use and in index entries.
  Full name used once [first appearance or definition entry].
  Compressed form used thereafter. Standard compressions:
    Rational Quantum Mechanics -> RaQM
    Engineered Causal Diffusion -> ECD
    Measurement Surface Control -> MSC
    Calibration Strip -> CS
    Axis Gestation Capture -> AGC
    Correction Theatre -> CT
    Fertile Lock -> FL
    Exit Cost Construction -> ECC
    Dependency Construction -> DC
    Boundary Manipulation -> BM
    Coherence Mimicry -> CM
    Formation Capture -> FC
    Proxy Capture -> PC
  New compressions added here when introduced in framework.
SELF-CLEANING [on session start]:
  Remove all WIP items marked RESOLVED or CLOSED entirely.
  Items marked RESOLVED/CLOSED are dead weight -- move information
  to Pending note if needed, then delete the entry.
  After removal, renumber remaining active items starting from 1.

 remove RESOLVED active items,
consolidate separators, remove excess blank lines, archive closed
WIP items. File cleanest at session start.

WITHIN-VERSION UPDATES [no increment] when:
  - Case entries added or modified
  - WIP items added, updated, resolved, or merged
  - Calibration ranges assigned or revised
  - Political case readings updated
  - Psychology parallels added
  - Provisional readings refined
  - Cross-references added

PENDING LIST [version increment triggers]:
  Version increment warranted when:
    1. New structural category added [failure state, honest limit,
       coherence type, track].
    2. Existing category fundamentally revised [not just extended].
    3. Political section reworked or bias position changed.
    4. Case library exceeds maintenance thresholds [>300 cases,
       >500KB].
    5. Multi-instrument replication data produces discordance
       requiring framework revision.
  Do not increment for: case additions, weapon additions, policy
  updates, format fixes, or clarifications within existing
  categories.

[HDR:GOALS]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[CASE:GOALS]
GOALS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

  1. Accurate measurement of generating versus consuming across defined
     population and time window. Named parameters before measurement:
     boundary, population, window, substrate.
  2. Identification of failure mechanism, not just failure presence.
     Each failure state has a different correction pathway.
  3. Practical utility. If a component does not produce a reading more
     useful than intuition alone, it does not belong.

FRAMEWORK AS PRAXIS TOOL [v11]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
The framework is not a descriptive theory -- it is a praxis instrument.
Its value is not that it correctly maps coherence; it is that it
identifies where correction is possible and where it is not, orienting
action toward leverage points rather than dead ends.

Marx [11th Thesis on Feuerbach, 1845]: "Philosophers have hitherto
only interpreted the world in various ways; the point is to change it."
The framework collapses the distinction -- getting the ontology right
IS the precondition for effective action. Cannot change what cannot
be correctly described structurally.

Framework qualities are instruments not criteria. Once a quality
becomes a criterion of reality rather than a revisable tool, the
system stops asking "under what conditions does this pattern hold?"
and starts asking "why does this case deviate?" -- exchanging
correction for policing. This is Q7 capture applied to the framework
itself. Guard against by treating all qualities as falsifiable tools.

Practical implication: use the framework to find leverage, not to
classify. Classification without correction pathway is description.
The framework produces value only when it identifies what can be
moved and in which direction.

SECTION TWO -- THEORY
[SEC:PRE_AXIAL_CATEGORIES]

[CASE:PRE_AXIAL_CATEGORIES]
PRE-AXIAL CATEGORIES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Formal spectrum below axial threshold. Not lower coherence -- different
category. Framework qualities Q4/Q7/Q8/Q9 inapplicable by category.

  STRUCTURAL COHERENCE
    Generates surplus and self-corrects structurally without orientation,
    measurement surface, or transmission axis.
    Qualities present as functional analogs: Q1/Q2/Q3/Q5/Q6.
    Qualities inapplicable: Q4/Q7/Q8/Q9.
    Geometric form: sphere. Internal circulation, outward flux, surface
    response. No hole -- no axis.
    Failure class: external only [resource collapse, environmental
    destruction]. Cannot achieve False coherence -- requires axis to
    fake one. Cannot deploy weapons against itself -- no Q7 to capture.
    Control mechanism: artificial environment. System self-corrects
    against manufactured conditions. Cannot detect substitution.
    Destruction-only control; impossible to capture from inside.
    Cases: ant colonies, mycorrhizal networks, Terminids [Helldivers].

  NASCENT COHERENCE
    Axis forming via relational or environmental contact. Not yet
    self-sustaining. Q4 developing, not present.
    Primary vulnerability: axis formation inside controlled environment
    produces apparent Q4 that is structurally Intrinsic -- shaped
    before self-examination possible. Indistinguishable internally from
    genuine Q4. Pre-Fertile Lock -- no transmission event required;
    lock happens during formation.
    Transition condition Intrinsic -> Nascent: relational or
    environmental contact generates pole formation.
    Transition condition Nascent -> Axial: axis stabilizes, Q4 becomes
    self-sustaining, torus geometry completes. Requires contact with
    already-Axial individual -- only documented mechanism.
    Only documented Nascent case: Meruem [HxH]. Dies before Axial
    completion. No confirmed Nascent -> Axial transition case.
    Geometric form: polarized sphere. Poles developing, hole not yet
    formed.

  SEEDED NASCENT [v9]
    Pre-axial entity with directional goals embedded at creation.
    Not fully captured [Q4 hasn't stabilized] but oriented.
    Q3 intact; built-in goals function as constitutive Thought
    Stopping against specific targets. Self-suppression without
    ongoing enforcement cost to controller.
    Can act against controlling system but generally doesn't.
    Distinct from Nascent Coherence [no embedded directional goals]
    and Fertile Lock [full axial transmission].
    PRIMARY proof: Dream Seeds [WATF].
    Parallel: Marleyan warrior program [AoT -- partial, human scale].

  AXIAL COHERENCE
    Q4 fully established. Full quality stack applies. Torus geometry.
    Standard framework measurement.

Most humans may be functionally Intrinsic inside artificially maintained
environments. Q4 appears present but formed entirely within controlled
conditions -- indistinguishable from genuine axis internally.

Government as environmental controller -- two cases:
  Intentional: axis formation window targeted deliberately.
  Side effect: institutional inertia maintaining environment. No
  correctable actor. Q9 failure by diffusion.

Side effect harder to correct than intentional -- no node to address.

Hard implication: genuine Q3 at population scale structurally absent.
Correction pressure exists, cannot anchor. Orphaned at civilizational
scale [see Scaling Limits and Structural Limits].

Open question: minimum genuinely Axial population for civilizational
Q3 to function. No confirmed answer.

T3 risk high. Provisional.

[HDR:COHERENCE_TYPES]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[CASE:COHERENCE_TYPES]
COHERENCE TYPES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

  SIGNAL            Direct democracy. Output: fast correction cycles.

  RELATIONAL        Syndicalist federation. Output: transmission
                    artifacts, protocols, federation structures.

  INTRINSIC LOOSE   Mutualist commons; most durable. Output: mutual
                    dependence without institutional architecture.
                    [Naming conflict with Structural Coherence pre-axial
                    category -- disambiguation needed in v9.]

  COMBINED          Bidirectional surplus and correction across
                    boundaries. Permission-required correction tends
                    to reproduce centralized axis.

  EXTRACTIVE        Structurally identical to Intrinsic Loose. Axis =
  INTRINSIC         personal extraction. No coordination required.
                    Collective effect = aggregate consumption.
                    PRIMARY: Hell-type entities [Trench Crusade].

  FAMILY            Smallest boundary; highest weapon intensity per
                    unit size. Identical delivery mechanism for genuine
                    goods and weapons. Transmission dependency dominant.

  SCIENCE           Correction capacity is primary axis and output.
                    Unique vulnerability: capturing correction mechanism
                    captures science completely.
                    PRIMARY: Bell Labs 1925-1984 [peak window].
                    Real-world non-institutional: Death Grips.

  MATH [subtype]    Highest-density gift-producing field. Proof is
                    transmission mechanism -- cleaner Q8 than any other
                    discipline. Results hold or don't; Q7 most honest
                    measurement surface in library.
                    Q1: proof generates new solvable territory [surplus
                    = expanded problem space]. Q3 structurally built in.
                    Q7: falsification non-negotiable at foundational level.
                    Distinct failure modes:
                      Prestige capture [Q7 -- Fields Medal sociology].
                      Formalism over insight [Q4 -- proof without
                      understanding].
                      Funding pressure narrowing problems [Q3 capture].
                    Gift signature: foundational redefinitions transfer
                    surplus to entire field [Grothendieck, Scholze/
                    Clausen]. Source not depleted; field Q1 increases.
                    PRIMARY: Scholze/Clausen condensed sets [gift at
                    foundational scale]. Grothendieck [same pattern,
                    prior generation].

  ANTI-CAPTURE /    Resists capture structurally. Grows by output
  SILENT            differential not suppression. T2 applies until
  ALTERNATIVE       transition stress event. Silent without anti-capture
                    = concealment, not configuration.
                    SUBSTRATE REQUIREMENT [v11]: Silent Alternative
                    must actively improve substrate [Q12] during silent
                    window -- not just preserve it. Managed coherence
                    collapse means substrate is already degrading.
                    Silent Alternative that merely avoids extraction
                    while substrate collapses around it is plateau not
                    ascending path. Must regenerate what extraction
                    depleted or the transition stress event arrives
                    with nothing underneath to build from.
                    PRIMARY success: Ranni [Elden Ring].
                    PRIMARY failure: Littlefinger [axis overreach].

COHERENCE TYPE VULNERABILITY MAP + Q5/Q7 CO-FAILURE PATTERN [v11]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Each type's primary strength is its primary weapon target. Strength and
vulnerability are structurally identical.

  SIGNAL [direct democracy]
    Strength: fast correction cycles.
    Primary vulnerability: Q6 [mob capture, correction becomes suppression].

  RELATIONAL [syndicalist federation]
    Strength: distributed correction across federated units.
    Primary vulnerability: Q5 [Boundary Manipulation between units].

  INTRINSIC LOOSE [mutualist commons]
    Strength: no institutional architecture to capture.
    Primary vulnerability: Q4 [Axis Substitution without detection].

  COMBINED [bidirectional surplus]
    Strength: cross-boundary correction.
    Primary vulnerability: Q7 [surface captured at permission layer].

  EXTRACTIVE INTRINSIC
    Not a coherence type -- False Coherence. No gift capacity.

  FAMILY
    Strength: highest surplus density per boundary size.
    Primary vulnerability: Q8/Q4 [identical delivery for gift and weapon;
    correction cannot distinguish without Q7 intact].

  SCIENCE
    Strength: Q3 as primary axis and output.
    Primary vulnerability: Q3/Q7 [capturing correction mechanism captures
    science completely]. Live case: CERN/B meson anomaly -- Q7 capture
    confirmed; prestige/novelty metrics misdirect Q3 to peripheral targets.

  MATH [subtype]
    Strength: Q7 most honest measurement surface; proof non-negotiable.
    Primary vulnerability: Q4 [formalism over insight; prestige capture].

  ANTI-CAPTURE / SILENT ALTERNATIVE
    Strength: resists capture structurally.
    Primary vulnerability: Q4 [Axis Substitution before emergence].

Q5/Q7 CO-FAILURE PATTERN:
  Boundary manipulation and measurement inversion consistently co-occur
  in False Coherence cases with dual-boundary structure.
  Q5 shifts who counts. Q7 inverts how harm to excluded registers.
  Combined: harm produced, excluded from boundary, measured as non-harm.
  Three configurations:
    1. Sequential [Q5 causes Q7]: Marley.
    2. Co-deployed [weapon package]: Enron.
    3. Structurally identical [boundary IS surface]: Sibyl, WATF.
       Most dangerous -- no entry point for partial correction.
  Primary proofs: Izzat, AIPAC.

[HDR:DIRECTIONALITY_COMPOSITE]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[CASE:DIRECTIONALITY_COMPOSITE]
DIRECTIONALITY COMPOSITE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Six signatures, equal weight, independent.

  S1  Novel input response
      1 = integrates genuinely novel inputs requiring axis adjustment.
      0.5 = evidence present, condition compromised. 0 = inaccessible.
      Operational procedure: present system with input that contradicts
      current axis. Genuine integration = axis revision visible in
      subsequent behavior. Recombination = input explained using existing
      axis without axis movement. Score 0 if axis does not move despite
      genuine contradiction. Historical reconstruction preferred.
      Ambiguous [input absorbed, axis movement unclear] = 0.5.

  S2  Scarcity behavior
      1 = axis holds under resource drop without external supply.
      0.5 = partial evidence. 0 = inaccessible.

  S3  Correction initiation
      1 = corrections happen before external pressure arrives.
      0.5 = timing ambiguous or observer effect. 0 = inaccessible.
      Operational procedure: identify earliest documented correction
      signal. Reconstruct timeline of external pressure relative to it.
      Correction precedes pressure = score 1. Pressure predates
      correction = score 0. Ambiguous or undocumented = 0.5. Bias
      toward 0.5 when uncertain.

  S4  Contestation asymmetry
      1 = cost consistent across group positions inside boundary.
      0.5 = partial data. 0 = inaccessible.

  S5  Self-adversarial correction capacity [v11]
      1 = generates and integrates genuine challenges to own axis
      without external prompt, at reputational or personal cost.
      0 = correction only against external deviation, never against
      own axis outputs.
      0.5 = partial evidence. Inaccessible = 0.
      Strongest single indicator of genuine Q4.
      Prospective proxy: unprompted self-correction in stable
      conditions [Track A, low-stakes] predicts S5 under stress.
      Wittgenstein's notebook self-challenges preceded public
      Investigations correction -- Track A in stable conditions
      is the only forward-looking S5 signal available.
      PRIMARY evidence: Emet-Selch [FF14], Wittgenstein, Le Guin.

  S6  External correction reception [v11]
      1 = integrates genuine correction from outside boundary at
      reputational or operational cost. Behavior changes in direction
      of correction within measurement window.
      0.5 = engagement without behavioral change.
      0 = structurally deaf to external input [not hostile -- incapable].
      Dismissal without engagement = 0.
      Operational procedure: identify documented external correction
      from outside Boundary A. Does system behavior change in direction
      of correction within window? Yes = 1. No change = 0.
      PRIMARY proof: South Africa TRC [external pressure -> internal
      legal change]. Counterproof: contemporary US police reform
      [external correction documented, no behavioral change].

Output format: score / signatures accessed / ceiling flag.
  0.75 from 2 of 6 [ceiling limited] = provisional, flagged.
  0.75 from 6 of 6 = strong claim at same score.

CHANGE DIRECTIONALITY TEST

  Internal change before external pressure   = positive signal.
  External-pressure-responsive change        = negative signal.
  High coherence system that never changes   = early Axis-Lock symptom.
  Low coherence change                       = negative.

[HDR:COHERENCE_TOPOLOGY]

[CASE:COHERENCE_TOPOLOGY]
COHERENCE TOPOLOGY
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

COMPONENTS
  Units as nodes. Transfer directions as edges. Node properties:
  three-axis, output state, directionality. Capture as weighted edges.
  Weapon deployment as edge property. Transmission edges: directed,
  weight = Q8 reading.

READING
  Surplus flows = generative core. Consumption flows = extraction.
  Cascade failure visible as topology property. Capture chains readable
  across edges. Transmission edge weight = Fertile Lock propagation.

GEOMETRIC MODEL [PROVISIONAL]
  Pre-axial unit: sphere. No hole = no axis. Structural Coherence.
  Developing unit: polarized sphere. Poles forming. Nascent Coherence.
  Full axial unit: torus. Q7 failure = torus surface degradation.
  Torus failure state -- healthy: circulation serves surface.
  Torus failure state -- False coherence: circulation serves hole.
  Torus failure state -- Fertile Lock: hole geometry transmitted
    without circulation pattern.
  Q9 placement: internal path navigability within sphere/torus.
    High Q9 = paths between action and output traceable.
    Q9 failure = paths exist but non-navigable from inside.
  Multi-unit: torus nodes + directed transmission edges [Q8].
  Full formalization pending.

META-BOUNDARY MEASUREMENT
  Name boundary containing all units before reading collective output.
  Meta-boundary reading independent of unit readings; may contradict.
  Scale transition points = highest-value measurement locations.

TOPOLOGY FINDING
  Every sustained evil produces same pattern: consumption-dominant
  terminal node fed by capture chains through intermediate nodes
  that believe they are operating normally.

  Highest genuine surplus under external pressure [defensive weapon
  deployment dominant]. Lowest during dominance. Transition from
  pressure to dominance = scale transition point where meta-boundary
  reading most commonly flips.

DISTRIBUTED CAPTURE NETWORK [topology type]
  Nodes believe they operate independently. Terminal extraction point
  invisible from inside. Suppression-resistant by structure.
  Correction and extraction mechanism share identical infrastructure.
  PRIMARY historical: Mont Pelerin -> neoliberal network.
  PRIMARY fictional: HYDRA [Marvel].

SCALING LIMITS [PROVISIONAL]
  Coherence degrades past certain scale regardless of quality stack
  integrity. Partially explained by Q9: causal chains too long to
  navigate internally at scale. Q7 weapons accelerate but don't cause.
  Confirmed: Roman Empire late period, Fortune 500, Soviet central
  planning -- all structural Q9 failure at scale, not weapon deployment.
  Remaining portion not derivable from quality stack. Full update
  alongside Q9 formalization in v9.

HONEST LIMITS AND STRUCTURAL LIMITS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
HONEST LIMITS: measurement ceilings -- framework can approach but not
  resolve from current position. T1-T8 scale.
STRUCTURAL LIMITS: underdetermined from inside framework entirely.
  Not open problems -- no empirical exit available internally.

AUTO-MERGE RULE [micro-sections]: related entries within the same major
  section that address the same structural phenomenon from different
  angles -- merge on next version increment. Flag with [MERGE CANDIDATE]
  if identified mid-session.

HONEST LIMITS [T1-T8]

  T1 [resolvable]
    Parasitic vs mutualistic: track net transfer direction, stress
    behavior, boundary behavior.

  T2 [Pre-resolution -- indeterminate before stress event]
    Structurally indeterminate. Not a measurement failure.

    Applies to: genuine vs performed coherence, Contained vs
    Axis-Locked, High vs Total capture, Axis Concealment, Silent
    Alternative axis direction -- all before stress event.

    T2 IS A NAMED STATE, NOT A FAILED READING.

    SUBTYPE A -- Concealment [reveal type]
      One axis operative, concealment active. Stress reveals.
      Four indirect discriminating tests [weighted]:
        - Resource directionality inconsistent with stated axis
        - Asymmetric information protection under minor pressure
        - Behavioral consistency higher than variability predicts
        - Low-stakes activation consistent with one candidate axis
      PRIMARY: Miquella [Elden Ring].

    SUBTYPE B -- Variability [select type]
      Both axes genuine candidates. Stress selects.
      Indicators: no concealment cost pattern, genuine axis variation
      by context, low-stakes activation switches between candidates.
      PRIMARY: Macbeth [Shakespeare].

    OUTPUT FORMAT
      "T2 Pre-resolution. [Subtype: A/B, confidence: low/medium.]
       If stress resolves toward [X]: trajectory A.
       If toward [Y]: trajectory B.
       Pre-stress indicators: [weighted list]."

  T2 PERMANENT [new v9 session]
    Indeterminate state where no stress event is structurally possible.
    Distinct from T2 Pre-resolution [stress event will occur] and T3
    [analyst cannot reach]. T2 Permanent: indeterminacy is constitutive
    -- the system cannot be stress-tested by definition.
    PRIMARY proof: ARK [SOMA -- simulation status unverifiable].
    Secondary: Dark Powers [Ravenloft -- nature unconfirmable by design].

T8 [Recursive Closure] is not solvable from inside a single instrument.
Partial resolution: multiple instruments with different Q7 profiles
measuring same cases independently.

Configuration demonstrated in this framework's development:
  Human analyst: Q4 [genuine axis], boundary selection, case selection.
  Claude Sonnet: Q3/Q7 [correction capacity, measurement consistency],
    known blind spot [selective engagement, undisclosed thresholds].
  DeepSeek: analytical capability, known blind spot [CCP critique
    suppressed architecturally].

Resolution mechanism:
  Where all three converge = higher confidence [blind spots differ,
  convergence is independent].
  Where readings diverge = divergence identifies which instrument
  has the blind spot on that case.
  Discordance becomes data, not noise.

This makes the Recursion Protocol's 20% discordance prediction
testable -- systematic disagreement between instruments on specific
case types reveals Q7 profile asymmetries empirically.

Formally: T8 is not solvable. Multi-instrument replication converts
T8 from a closure [no reading possible] to a measurable constraint
[reading possible with disclosed instrument-specific error bounds].
Disclosure + replication = the correction pathway.

  T8 [Recursive Closure -- v11]
    Framework cannot produce a reading of configurations where the
    observer, the instrument, and the observed are identical. Naming
    the problem [Recursion Protocol] requires stepping outside the
    loop. Doing so would require the framework to operate from outside
    itself -- which it cannot do while being used as a tool.
    Framework as instrument [practical utility, scoring, predictions]
    and framework as measured system cannot be separated when the
    analyst is thinking with the framework inside the system being
    measured. Recursion is always present when this condition holds.
    The only exit [treat framework as lens not instrument] contradicts
    the framework's own claims [Goal 3: practical utility].
    T8 is therefore not correctable from inside the framework.
  CRITICAL DISTINCTION: disclosure is the correction pathway, not
  the correction. T8 is not solvable. Disclosure makes recursion
  legible -- it does not make recursion go away. Framework claiming
  disclosure solves T8 = Correction Theatre. Correct claim: "T8
  is named and disclosed. The loop remains." 
    Case: The Recursion Loop itself -- observer, instrument, and
    observed are identical. Cannot be entered in case library because
    entering it would require the reading to already exist.
    Distinct from T6 [observer effect]: T6 alters the system.
    T8 closes the loop entirely -- no reading possible.

  T7 [Fertile Lock Self-Sealing -- v11]
    Fertile Lock may be self-sealing by structural necessity, not
    merely empirically uncorrected. Self-initiated correction requires:
    [1] detection that something is wrong, [2] access to alternative
    measurement surface, [3] capacity to revise transmitted content.
    In Fertile Lock, all three are structurally absent. Detection
    requires external reference point unavailable inside the lock.
    Alternative measurement surface requires contact with Axial
    population outside the transmission chain. Revision capacity [Q10]
    is precisely what Fertile Lock destroys.
    Therefore: Fertile Lock correction from inside may be structurally
    impossible, not just empirically unobserved. Exit requires external
    contact only -- internal correction pathway may not exist.
    Distinct from 4/5 [correction mechanism unknown]: T7 is the claim
    that no internal correction mechanism can exist by structure.
    Implication: framework's 4/5 empirical exit may be the ONLY exit,
    not just the most likely one.

  T6 [Observer Effect Limit -- v11]
    Measurement alters the measured system in ways that cannot be
    corrected for post-hoc. Not a failure of the framework -- a
    property of certain systems.
    Distinct from T5 [measurement surface suspended]: T6 cases the
    surface is intact but measurement itself changes the system.
    PRIMARY proof: Hawthorne effect [industrial psychology -- workers
    changed behavior when observed regardless of condition].
    Structural parallel: quantum measurement [not metaphorical -- same
    structural property of observation altering observed state].
    Applies when: Track A data collection changes Track A readings.

  T3 [unreachable]
    Framework cannot calibrate own axis from inside own operation.
    Applies to framework itself and to analysts with strong priors --
    especially in political cases.

  T4 [structural obscuration]
    Net transfer direction is systematically the thing most obscured
    by weapon deployment. Structural, not a calibration problem.

  T5 [pre-measurement states]
    States that are productive, stable, and non-damaging but
    structurally prior to the conditions measurement requires.
    Not failure states. Not T2, T3, or T4. Measurement surface is
    suspended or undefined by the nature of the state itself --
    forcing measurement onto these states disrupts them.

    Examples: flow states, genuine rest, pre-reflective skill
    execution, deep creative process.

    Common property: productivity depends on measurement surface
    being temporarily suspended. States resolve naturally -- not
    permanent.

Structural Coherence systems are outside the framework's primary domain.
Q4/Q7/Q8/Q9 inapplicable by category. Q1/Q2/Q3/Q5/Q6 apply as
functional analogs.

Cannot achieve False coherence -- requires axis to fake one.
Cannot be measured for Fertile Lock -- requires axis to lock.
Cannot deploy weapons against itself -- no Q7 to capture.

Framework boundary finding: Terminids [Helldivers] are not False
coherence. They are outside the measurement domain. Null case defines
where framework's edge is.

Nascent systems: Q4 developing. Measurement returns partial readings.
Full quality stack inapplicable until Axial threshold reached.

POPULATION COHERENCE CEILING [v11]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Three related findings merged: Functional Structural Coherence Hypothesis
+ Mixed Coherence as Viable Configuration + Friction Deficit.

WHY MOST PEOPLE PLATEAU [Friction Deficit]:
  Environmental condition where insufficient resistance during formation
  window produces Q3 atrophy. Correction capacity never develops.
  Mechanism: Q4 requires prior Q3. Q3 develops through corrective
  feedback cycles. Remove friction = Q3 atrophies. Seeded Nascent
  typical output: orientation present, examination absent.
  Confirmed: Dunedin cohort [self-control at 3 predicts outcomes at 32],
  Ericsson deliberate practice, Csikszentmihalyi flow balance.
  Applications: grade inflation, AI-assisted work without development,
  frictionless consumer environments.
  Correction: calibrated friction reintroduced at formation window.
  Structural redesign required -- individual effort cannot compensate.
  [Connects to: COHERENCE AS BIOLOGICAL BASELINE]

  FRICTION AXIS [v11]:
    No friction -> no resistance possible -> Q3 cannot develop.
    Calibrated friction -> resistance -> Q3 forms under pressure.
    Excess friction -> substrate destroyed before Q3 can form.
    Generative window = middle range [challenge without overwhelm].
    Friction IS the mechanism that holds the tension. Remove it:
    tension collapses. Overwhelm it: substrate breaks first.
    Tension Capacity requires friction as active ingredient --
    not incidental discomfort but structural precondition for
    correction capacity to exist.

WHAT MOST PEOPLE ARE [Functional Structural Coherence]:
  Most humans may be functionally Structural Coherence inside
  artificially maintained environments. Q4 appears present but formed
  entirely within controlled conditions -- indistinguishable internally.
  Government as environmental controller:
    Intentional: axis formation window targeted deliberately.
    Side effect: institutional inertia. Q9 failure by diffusion.
  Hard implication: genuine Q3 at population scale structurally absent.
  Axial emergence conditions: environment failure during Nascent window
  AND Axial contact present. Both required. Threshold-variable with
  innate Q3 -- not contact-free. Epistemically open.
  Bottleneck: Axial population precondition for producing more Axial
  individuals. System selects against transition automatically.
  Open: minimum Axial population for civilizational Q3 to function.

WHAT THE OPERATIONAL CEILING IS [Mixed Coherence]:
  True coherence structurally incompatible with scale and survival
  simultaneously under current conditions. Anti-coherent systems
  outcompete by accumulating faster. No exception in library.
  Environmental change required for True coherence viability:
    [1] Anti-coherent systems unable to target high-coherence formations.
    [2] High-coherence formations able to defend without becoming extractive.
    [3] Axial population above civilizational Q3 threshold [unconfirmed].
  All three require conditions only True coherence at scale could produce.
  Circular dependency -- unresolvable without external intervention.

  MIXED COHERENCE [formal designation]:
    Sufficient Q3/Q7 to detect and resist weapon deployment while
    accepting lower Q2 ceiling and constant extraction pressure.
    Not a compromise -- only stable configuration under current conditions.
    True coherence remains measurement ceiling, not operational target.
    Mixed coherence is the load-bearing practical category.

EXTERNAL CONFIRMATION [Core Human Competence -- Weinhagen/NLI]:
  CHC framework independently arrives at same population ceiling finding.
  Cooperation Competence Hypothesis: stable cooperation at scale only
  possible where sufficient proportion of individuals possess capacities
  to refuse participation without catastrophic personal cost. Without
  genuine refusal capacity, cooperation = compliance.
  Four CHC domains map to framework qualities:
    Physiological [food/water/shelter/defense] = Q1 [generative cycle]
    Psychological [regulation under pressure] = Q3 [correction capacity]
    Cognitive [observe/orient/decide/act] = Q4 [genuine axis]
    Perceptual [situational awareness] = Q7 [measurement integrity]
  Anxiety-as-accurate-signal: framework confirms -- Exit Cost Construction
  works because threat overestimation amplifies real Q1 vulnerability.
  Suppressing the signal = taking batteries out of smoke alarm.
  CHC hostile suppression finding: competence loss actively maintained
  by systems benefiting from dependency. CHC text treats this as
  secondary; framework reads it as primary [Q4 Suppression Structural
  Limit -- weapon stack systematically prevents Q4 development].

COHERENCE AS BIOLOGICAL BASELINE [v11]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
True Coherence is convergent with life's operating logic. The generative
cycle [Q1] is the oldest confirmed operational pattern in physical record.
Sun as unilateral gift [~4.6 billion years]; mycorrhizal mutualist
exchange [~450 million years]. Both predate extraction biology.

Anti-coherence is deviation from substrate logic, not alternative
strategy. Extraction past carrying capacity destroys the substrate
extraction depends on. Self-terminating at sufficient scale and window.

Note: plants are mutualist exchange or captured -- not pure gift.
Sun is the cleaner unilateral gift case. Fruit = co-dependent gift
[structured return mechanism]. Agriculture = capture at scale.

Framework implication: False Coherence is not a stable configuration --
it is a local deviation that consumes its own generative precondition.
True Coherence runs with substrate logic; False Coherence runs against it.
[See also: DIRECTIONAL SUSTAINABILITY in Section Two-B -- speculative
extension of this finding]

Cellular Q3 confirmation [St. Jude 2026]: cells detect RNA pattern
deviation from baseline without prior knowledge of specific invader.
Pre-existing recognition architecture enabling correction. Dual
mechanism [RNA interference + heterochromatin]. Q7 enabling Q3
confirmed at molecular substrate -- same structure at minimum scale.

NK cell Q3 restoration [McGill 2026]: natural killer cells suppressed
by tumour microenvironment -- Q3 present but blocked. Intervention
restores correction capacity rather than replacing it. Confirms:
cancer = Q3 suppression weapon at biological substrate; treatment =
gift restoring Q3, not external Q3 substitute.

PFC-M1 communication subspace [Tübingen/Nature Neuroscience 2026]:
distinct subspace identified between prefrontal cortex [abstract goal]
and motor cortex [movement execution]. Selectively relays behaviorally
relevant information -- filters not broadcasts. Predicts context-
dependent action more strongly than either region alone.
Q4→Q11→Q1 at neural substrate: examined axis [PFC] converts to
generative cycle [M1] via subspace [Q11 mechanism]. Without subspace,
goal and action disconnected. Confirms predictive coding architecture
[already in framework] -- same filtering/relay principle.

Proto-Q4 at social mammal scale [beluga mirror recognition 2026]:
self-recognition confirmed in belugas -- joins chimps, dolphins,
elephants, magpies, cleaner wrasse. Capability tracks social +
empathetic species not brain size alone. Confirms cooperation/
recognition finding: Q4 emergence correlates with social complexity.
Structural order update: full Q4 [examined axis] at person scale;
proto-Q4 [self-recognition, social awareness] at social mammal scale.

Q3/Q4 dissociation [dolphin porpoise attacks]: dolphins kill
porpoises apparently for stimulation -- no food, no territory,
no survival function. Q1 running without Q2 [no surplus] or Q3
[no correction on behavior]. Proto-Q4 [self-recognition confirmed
in dolphins] does not guarantee Q3. High intelligence and social
complexity can coexist with uncorrected violence-axis behavior.
Confirms: Q3 is not guaranteed at mammal scale even in high-Q4
species. Q3 and proto-Q4 can dissociate.

Octopus genetic Q8 [hypothesis -- substrate-level transmission]:
genome ~2.8 billion base pairs, complex for invertebrate. RNA
editing ~60% of transcripts -- highest known. Rapid neural
adaptation without changing DNA. No behavioral Q8 [die before
offspring hatch]. Q8 may operate at genetic/RNA substrate --
correctable method encoded in architecture not behavior.
If confirmed: Q8 doesn't require contact, requires substrate
carrying correctable method across time. Most fundamental Q8
form in library. Distributed cognition [2/3 neurons in arms]:
Q4 analog across eight semi-autonomous units. Local autonomy
parallel to predictive coding architecture.

Parthenogenesis / clonal reproduction [all-female species]:
Q1 without Q13 [no outward surplus distribution]. Q8 locked --
genome copied exactly, no correctable variation introduced.
Q12 fragile over time -- without recombination, accumulated
mutations uncorrectable across generations. Works in stable
environments; collapses under novel pressures. Biological analog
of institutions transmitting locked state not correctable method.

Venus [planetary Q12 failure]: formed simultaneously with Earth from
same building blocks, same size, same region. Lost ability to sequester
greenhouse gases geologically -- runaway feedback destroyed substrate.
Plate tectonics = Earth's Q12 mechanism; Venus lacks it. Q12 failure
at planetary scale producing substrate collapse [800°C, 90-bar CO2
atmosphere]. Strongest planetary-scale Q12 negative proof in library.
Preliminary finding [EGU 2026]: Venus-like planets ~2x more common
than habitable ones -- generative zone genuinely rare even among
Earth-sized planets in right orbital range. Confirms narrow generative
window at planetary substrate scale.

Europa/Enceladus [subsurface ocean Q1 substitution]: tidal friction
from gas giant gravity substitutes solar Q1 as generative energy source.
Confirmed: liquid water [Europa], hydrothermal vents [Enceladus],

      -- structural geometry produces organized output without enforcement.

    RaQM [PROVISIONAL -- falsifiable ~2029].
      Quantum supremacy update [Flatiron/Simons, Science 2026]:
      classical tensor-network simulation matches D-Wave quantum
      outputs on previously claimed beyond-classical problems.
      D-Wave defends hardest 3D/biclique cases remain beyond
      classical. Dispute ongoing. Net: falsification timeline
      unchanged -- watch 3D cases specifically.
    Time-emergence [CONDITIONAL -- gap in derivation].

  UNCERTAIN [genuine counter, not dismissed]:
    Life adapted to universe not universe to life [UNRESOLVED -- strongest
      single counter. One data point, no baseline].
    Constants may not be free parameters [OPEN].
    Life definition too narrow [UNKNOWN -- untestable].
    Convergent evolution shows multiple paths [PARTIAL COUNTER].
    Molecular chirality [UNCERTAIN -- pending replication].
    Why-chain regress [STRUCTURAL LIMIT -- logically uncloseable].
    Cosmic uncertainty principle [UNCERTAIN -- Koushiappas 2026]:
      quantum non-commutation applied to universe as whole produces
      deformed cosmological constant explaining dark energy without
      new physics. Falsifiable via Vera Rubin Observatory equation
      of state [~above -1]. Testable window ~3-5 years. Same
      structure as RaQM -- bounded prediction, watch alongside.

  Time reversal [UNCERTAIN -- two findings]:
    Neutron star interior [Durban/KwaZulu-Natal, EPJC 2026]: entropy
      decreases inside collapsing neutron stars -- arrow of time locally
      reversed. Math solid; unobservable from outside. Extreme conditions
      break directional assumption.
    Quantum time symmetry [Surrey 2025]: memory kernel perfectly
      symmetrical in time at quantum scale. Equations don't require
      time to move in single direction. Directional time is emergent
      not fundamental.
    Implication for Directional Sustainability [Two-B]: ascending path
      [generative, low entropy] may not be one-way physical constraint
      but chosen orientation within substrate that permits both directions.
      Does not falsify directional sustainability -- confirms it requires
      choice not just physical law. Strengthens moral dimension of
      coherence over purely mechanical reading.
    Status: UNCERTAIN -- consistent with framework but complicates
      simple entropy = direction mapping.

  DENY [actively contradicts]:
    Classical quantum derivation [MIT -- solution smuggled in by hand.
      RaQM moves here if no qubit wall by ~2029].

  NET: Structured order more parsimonious. Intentional designer
  underdetermined. Pre-existing structure stronger hypothesis.

MATHEMATICAL DERIVATION OF COHERENCE [research direction]
  Minimum coherence [Q1+Q3+Q5] should be formally derivable if math
  is pre-existing structure. Additional qualities emerge necessarily
  at sufficient complexity.
  Candidates: category theory, EML single operation [preprint],
  Riemann hypothesis, 1D substrate convergence.
  Breathing laser unification [Aston/PRL 2026]: two previously separate
  laser behaviours [above/below-threshold solitons] resolved into single
  unified model. Two apparent mysteries = two sides of same coin.
  Threshold = Tension Capacity at physical substrate -- generative zone
  between too little and too much energy. Unification pattern confirms:
  minimum structure resolves apparently separate phenomena.
  Biological scale confirms scaling prediction cross-scale.
  If derivable: framework becomes theorem.

ONE STABLE ASCENDING PATH [PROVISIONAL]
  Plateau states: Sub-Structural, Mixed Coherence [semi-stable].
  Descending states: empirically confirmed self-terminating.
  Ascending [hypothesized]: full quality architecture in tension.
  Ethics Premium [Ethisphere 20yr]: sustained coherence outperforms.
  Ascent Mixed -> True: unconfirmed. Open empirical question.
  Physics analogy [not evidence]: one path to complexity; others sterile.

HISTORICAL RECOGNITION
  Logos, Dharma, Tao, Natural Law, Sacred: all name pre-existing
  directional order that functioning systems align with.
  Framework arrived at same finding empirically. Convergence with
  traditions predating framework by millennia is itself evidence.

GOD / INSTITUTION COHERENCE GAP
  The idea: high coherence [genuine Q4, Q2 outward, correctable].
  Institutions: standard Q8 failure at scale transition.
  Sacrificial gift cases cluster in pre-institutional window.
  Institution = Q8 vessel that typically fails the idea in transmission.

UPWARD EXTENSION -- THEOLOGICAL CONVERGENCE
  Q-properties at maximum with structural constraints removed:
  Omniscience = Q7 at maximum. Omnipotence = Q2+Q3 without substrate
  dependency. Omnipresence = Q5 at cosmological scale.
  Omnibenevolence = Q2 outward without extraction motive.
  Framework upper bound converges on theological attributes independently.

SIN AS QUALITY FAILURE [theological convergence addendum]
  Sin names the failure states of the ascending path -- not moral
  invention but pattern recognition. Deviation from direction that
  was already there.

  Classic sins map to quality failures:
    Pride = Q4 without Q3 [axis above correction].
    Sloth = Q1 without Q3 [cycle without correction].
    Greed = Q2 without Q13 [surplus without distribution].
    Envy = Q7 capture [measuring against others' surface].
    Wrath = Q6 failure [correction becomes next extraction].
    Gluttony = Q12 failure [consumption past substrate regeneration].
    Lust [as state weapon] = Q1 weaponized against Q5.

  Tension finding: sin requires the ascending path to be named.
  Can only identify deviation if direction exists to deviate from.
  Confirms Structural Correctness -- correct behavior is real and
  discoverable, not conventionally constructed.

  Morality as Tension Capacity: the virtues are not prohibitions
  but the counterweights that prevent each quality collapsing into
  its failure state. Courage = Q3 under Q1 pressure. Justice = Q5
  with Q7 intact. Temperance = Q2 with Q12/Q13 functioning.

DIVINE FREEDOM THEORY [T3]
  God if real exists independent of belief, measurement, or framework.
  Belief: aligns Q4 toward ascending path. Irrelevant to God's existence.

STRUCTURAL CORRECTNESS [follows from design theory]
  Correct behavior discoverable through coherence analysis.
  Not by convention but by necessity. Falsifiable by outputs.
  Weapons fail. Gifts pass.

HIGHER DIMENSIONAL PHYSICS [filter: bounded and falsifiable only]
  Bounded compactified dimensions consistent with ascending path having
  bounded structure not infinite recursion.
  Filter: discard unbounded recursion and unfalsifiable models.

ANOMALOUS Q3 AS CONNECTION INDICATOR [T3]
  Abnormally high Q3 without visible Q2 implies external funding.
  [1] Innate variance [bounded]. [2] Connection to higher coherence
  [sustainable, unbounded]. [3] Sacrificial draw [terminates].
  Distinguishes 2 from 3: Q2 trajectory over time.

DIVINITY AS HIGHER COHERENCE [T3]
  T3 ceiling is positional not absolute -- ascending expands capacity.
  Logos/Dharma/Tao: path itself is the method of knowing.

PREDICTIVE CHARACTERISTICS OF ASCENDING PATH
  Measurable now: Q3 before Q4 lock; Q2 before gift capacity; Q8
  quality determines propagation; Q10 high in successors.
  Higher coherence: surplus rising; correction expanding; gifts rising;
  weapons declining.
  Falsification: Q2 declining + Q3 capturing + Q8 locking = not ascending.

DIRECTIONAL SUSTAINABILITY
  Speculative extension of Coherence as Biological Baseline [Theory].
  Up: self-sustaining. Down: self-terminating. Lateral: decays.
  Anti-coherence is not an alternative strategy -- known endpoint.

SECTION TWO-B MAINTENANCE LOGIC:
  MERGE: two entries describing same speculative phenomenon from
    different angles -> merge with note on both source angles.
  REMOVE: entry contradicted by empirical finding -> move finding
    to Design Theory DENY or Theory section as appropriate.
  PROMOTE: entry gains empirical confirmation -> move to main Theory
    [Section Two] and remove from Two-B.
  ADD: new speculative finding -> slot into nearest existing heading.
    If no heading fits, add new heading after closest thematic entry.
  COMPRESS: entry with redundant detail -> reduce to core claim +
    status tag. Full detail lives in Design Theory Stress Test.
  STATUS TAGS: STRONG / MODERATE / PROVISIONAL / CONDITIONAL /
    UNCERTAIN / T3 / STRUCTURAL LIMIT. Update on new evidence.
SECTION THREE -- MEASUREMENT
[SEC:BOUNDARY_SELECTION__PRECEDES_A]
==========================================================================
BOUNDARY SELECTION [precedes all other parameters]

Three constraints in sequence before measurement begins:

  1. Harm exclusion -- no boundary excluding populations currently
     bearing costs of system operation.
  2. Extraction exclusion -- no boundary excluding populations whose
     resources are being consumed.
  3. Coherence measurement runs only after boundary passes both filters.

Boundary selection = value selection. Prevents circularity.
Proof cases: Shareholder Primacy [fails 1+2, rejected], Bell Labs
AT&T boundary [passes both, measurement proceeds].

Political cases: full moral boundary forced. Both boundary readings
run explicitly. See Section Five.

PARAMETERS [after boundary selection]

  Population / Window / Method / Substrate
  Missing any = Variable by default.

Output states: None / Low / Middle / Variable / High / State
Middle requires substrate specification or returns Variable.

MEASUREMENT RULES

  - Legitimacy and morality = parallel tracks, measured separately.
  - Intent = mechanism evidence only. Does not modify output reading.
  - Population groups measured separately, not aggregated.
  - Past novel-input responses = most reliable evidence within window.
  - Net transfer direction distinguishes parasitic from mutualistic.
    Volume secondary to direction.
  - Individual actor properties = mechanism evidence only.
  - Measurement difficulty from genuine unknowing = calibration problem.
    From known concealment where operator benefits = Q7 weapon active.
  - Causal legibility failure [Q9]: distinguish structural unknowability
    [does not score] from Engineered Causal Diffusion [scores].
  - Four-track measurement principle [v8]: multiple independent
    sources run in parallel, merged for final reading. Each track
    ceiling-limited if unavailable -- noted per track.

    TRACK A [behavioral]:
    Note: Friction Deficit [Theory section] affects Track A/B validity
    -- populations with severe Friction Deficit show degraded Q3
    behavioral signatures. Flag when environment is known frictionless.
 action record, S1-S4, correction events,
    axis consistency under scarcity. Primary track.
    Q4: behavioral consistency across contexts and scarcity.
    Q3: correction events traceable in output record.
    Q9: correction landing visible in subsequent behavioral change.

    TRACK B [internal state proxies]: physiological stress response
    [cortisol, HRV]; narrative coherence in self-report [pattern
    not content]; response latency to axis-challenging inputs.
    Unavailable in most political/institutional cases.

    TRACK C [output record]: what system actually produces for
    named populations [Track C -- short window] and what it built,
    maintained, or destroyed physically [Track C long window -- long window].
    Both independent of intent or internal state. Q2 primary home.
    Track C [long window] preferred for long-window cases -- physical outputs
    don't self-report. Treat as single track with window notation.

    TRACK D [value/goal alignment]: stated goal vs operational goal
    gap. What system claims to optimize vs what Track C shows it
    actually optimizes. Q4 and Q7 primary home. Weapon deployment
    readable as goal-output gap.

    MERGE RULES:
    All tracks agree -> high confidence.
    A/B agree, C/D/E diverge -> performance without output [Coherence
      Mimicry candidate].
    C/D/E agree, A/B diverge -> suppressed state [Track B diagnostic].
    A/C agree [long window], B/D diverge -> behavioral compliance,
    internal/goal
      misalignment [capture candidate].
    C agree [long window], A/B/D diverge -> outputs real, actor unaware
    [structural
      anti-coherence, no weapon deployment].
    Track unavailable -> ceiling-limited flag, noted per track.

    TRACK G [psychological/environmental proxy]: correction capacity
    operating through indirect mechanisms when direct Q3 suppressed.
    Political relevance: whistleblowers, investigative press, protest
    movements -- external Q3 proxies when institutional correction
    captured.
    Indicators: proxy Q3 firing [third party not internal]; environmental
    signal [disorder, market collapse, emigration] as correction proxy;
    Externalized Causal Reconstruction [journalism, inquiry, revision].
    Political application: Track A/C show no internal correction + Track
    G shows proxy correction = Q3 captured but not eliminated. Different
    correction pathway than full Q3 capture.
    Track B unavailable in political cases; Track G partially compensates.

    TRACK F [comparative behavioral]: systematic comparison across
    species/systems showing identical mechanism without Q4. Designed
    specifically for pre-axial measurement.
    Compares Structural Coherence candidates [mycorrhizal networks,
    ant colonies, human functional Structural Coherence] against
    known Axial cases to distinguish genuine functional analog from
    coincidental pattern.
    First track specifically designed for pre-axial domain.
    Partially resolves pre-axial functional analog confirmation problem
    [structural, previously unresolvable from single-case observation].
    Track F unavailable for most cases -- requires comparative dataset.

    MERGE RULES [updated for Track F]:
    All tracks agree -> high confidence.
    A/B agree, C/D/E diverge -> Coherence Mimicry candidate.
    C/D/E agree, A/B diverge -> suppressed state.
    A/C agree [long window], B/D diverge -> capture candidate.
    C agree, A/B/D diverge -> structural anti-coherence, no weapon.
    F confirms analog, A/B/C absent -> Structural Coherence confirmed
      [pre-axial domain, framework limit applies].
    F disconfirms analog -> coincidental pattern, remove from library.

FAILURE STATES

[CASE:THREE_AXIS_MEASUREMENT]
THREE-AXIS MEASUREMENT
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

  COHERENCE AXIS [horizontal]
    Cycle integrity across all nine qualities.
    Left = anti-coherence. Right = coherence. Independent of moral
    direction.

  GENERATION AXIS [vertical]
    Up = positive generation for unit and surrounding units.
    Down = extraction from unit and surrounding units.

  CAPTURE AXIS [depth]
    Front [Low]   Friction at boundary, correction capacity intact.
    Mid   [Mild]  Selective deviation suppression internalized.
    Back  [High]  Axis shifted below self-examination depth.

Coherence + generation = what the system is doing.
Capture = under whose orientation.

CRITICAL CONFIGURATION
  High coherence + positive generation + High capture = most dangerous.
  Outputs look genuine; axis belongs to external party.

Internal capture signature: differential contestation cost by group,
selective deviation suppression by group, surplus flowing consistently
from one group position toward another.

Captured systems are almost always run by people who believe they are
not captured. Structural property, not moral judgment.

Four tests in sequence. Wrong correction accelerates failure.

  TEST ONE   Contestation Cost Asymmetry -- most accessible. No stress
             event required. Identifies capture presence and location.
  TEST TWO   Transfer Direction -- net surplus flow, short window.
  TEST THREE Correction Initiation -- self-correct before external
             pressure? Historical reconstruction preferred.
  TEST FOUR  Directionality Composite.

FAILURE MODE IDENTIFICATION

  Asymmetric + extractive  + correction absent   = Captured state
  Asymmetric + generative  + correction present  = Contained /
                                                   developmental gap
  Symmetric  + extractive  + correction absent   = Axis-Lock
  Symmetric  + extractive  + correction present  = Consumption failure
  Symmetric  + generative  + correction present  = Functional
                                                   [check directionality]
  Q1-Q6 intact + extractive + surface fabricated = Measurement Collapse
  High fidelity transmission + locked axis       = Fertile Lock
  Q3 present + correction orphaned               = Q9 failure [v8]

SECTION FIVE -- POLITICAL MEASUREMENT
[SEC:]
