[HDR:FRAMEWORK_VALUES_AND_POSITIONS]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
FRAMEWORK VALUES AND POSITIONS [Mutual Ordoliberal Republican -- v11]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Label: Mutual Ordoliberal Republican.
Historical proof: West Germany 1949-1970s. Subverted by
financialization and EU harmonization.
Instrument Q7 profile: human analyst + Claude Sonnet [iterative
co-development]. Prior declared: subsidiarity positive reference
throughout. T3 applies to all framework-derived positions.

  DERIVED VALUE PRINCIPLES [from position pattern]:

    1. Q3 must have teeth or it does not exist.
       Personal liability, use-or-lose, serious enforcement,
       military correction mechanism, difficult thresholds --
       all reject Correction Theatre [appearance of correction
       without consequence]. If the mechanism cannot fire with
       real effect, it is not a mechanism.

    2. Boundary clarity before power is granted.
       Family vs nation, state vs individual, local vs federal
       -- boundary must be defined before authority is assigned,
       not negotiated after. Vague boundaries = weapon surface.

    3. Extraction is the primary evil, not inequality.
       Flat tax, use-or-lose IP, anti-concentration, anti-H-visa
       structure, use-or-lose land -- all target the mechanism
       that takes without generating. Differential outcomes are
       not the problem. Taking without producing is.

    4. Proportionality as structural requirement.
       Every mechanism calibrated to harm level -- death
       penalty very difficult, forfeiture difficult process,
       emergency powers narrow and time-limited, state
       intervention high threshold. Rejects both underresponse
       [Correction Theatre] and overresponse [weapon usage].
       Q6 [proportional response] as load-bearing value not
       procedural preference.

  TENSION RESOLUTIONS:

    Parental authority vs education enforcement:
      State enforces minimum education -- absolute freedom
      not possible. High threshold elsewhere, education
      is the defined exception.

    Population stability:
      Sustainability is the primary goal. If birth rate
      drops, pro-natalist policy preferred over immigration
      expansion. Population decline addressed at source.

    Inheritance taxation:
      Total value taxed regardless at flat rate. Family
      boundary is not a tax exemption -- inheritance is
      a value transfer, taxed as any other.

    Death penalty non-weapon design:
      Jury + judge supermajority required. Long hold
      with rapid retry option. If retry fails, wait until
      natural death -- execution only after exhausted
      process. Simple fast methods only. Basic weapons
      and civil speech protected regardless of charge.
      Overreaction is the primary risk to manage.

    Building rights under national land:
      State apparatus enforces building rights as defined
      by constitution. Constrained state still enforces
      within its defined scope.

ARCHITECTURE:
  Political: distributed power [Swiss cantonal subsidiarity].
  Economic: Ordoliberal [markets yes, concentration no].
  Founding: Georgist land value tax [Calibration Strip correction].
  Voting: earned standing [tax requirement, temporary felony
    removal, competence threshold -- must be capture-resistant].

FOUNDING PRINCIPLES:
  Tax: legitimate Q5 cost when funds Q2 for taxed population.
  Land theft: Calibration Strip at civilizational scale. Death
    spiral origin. Georgist correction most structurally honest.
  Rights vs enforcement: architecturally independent. Rights = Q4.
    Enforcement = Q3. Correction Theatre without both.
  Monopoly: coherent when nation-approved and genuinely useful.
    Fails when approval mechanism captured.
  Violence: death spiral mechanism. Legitimate only when
    non-violent correction structurally unavailable.

POLICY POSITIONS [framework-derived]:
  Term limits:
    One term, mandatory sit-out, re-eligible after gap.
    Shifting between positions [executive to legislative]
    permitted -- prevents Axis-Lock in specific roles while
    retaining institutional knowledge.
    Framework basis: pro-competition framing not anti-
    incumbent. Same logic applies to government as market
    -- concentration without rotation = extraction gradient.

  Competition [government and people]:
    Pro-competition as primary value across both domains.
    Government: distributed power, term rotation, no
    permanent incumbency. People: equal access to entry,
    no structural barriers protecting existing position
    holders. Private motivation is separate -- competition
    structures the field, doesn't dictate internal drive.

  Prison / sentencing:
    Short sentences until correction demonstrated, then
    release. Failure to correct = very long sentence.
    Framework basis: Q3 test -- is correction capacity
    present and firing? If yes, release serves Q9 repair.
    If no, containment protects Boundary B.
    Death sentence: available but very difficult threshold
    [high evidence bar, independent review mandatory].
    Mandatory time delay after sentencing before execution
    -- reduces weapon use of death penalty [false
    conviction correction window].

  Unions:
    Good, non-mandatory. Q3 instrument for labor boundary --
    collective correction capacity against employer extraction.
    Mandatory membership = Structural Irrationality Mandate.
    Open shop preserves individual Q5 while maintaining
    collective Q3 availability.

  Land:
    Nation owns all land. Makes government Q5 integrity
    critical -- land ownership concentration is impossible
    but government capture becomes the primary extraction
    risk. Georgist land value tax is transitional mechanism;
    full national ownership is the structural endpoint.
    Implication: government quality is not optional under
    this model. Bad government = total extraction capacity.

  UBI / welfare:
    Welfare through infrastructure and direct provision
    [housing, healthcare, food, transport] not cash.
    Backup not lifestyle -- sufficient for survival, not
    comfort. Prevents destitution without creating
    permanent dependency. Cash transfers captured or
    misused; direct provision harder to extract from.

  Nuclear / energy infrastructure:
    Reactor technology good -- highest energy density, lowest
    land use, lowest lifecycle emissions. Safety requirements
    non-negotiable. Personnel accountability: any failure in
    critical infrastructure [nuclear, grid, water, transport]
    triggers named individual enforcement targets not just
    institutional fines. Applies to any tech or infrastructure
    where failure has population-scale consequences.
    Framework basis: institutional fines = Correction Theatre
    [cost absorbed, behavior unchanged]. Personal liability
    = Q3 firing at the operator level.

  Electoral design [multiple options -- no strong position]:
    Core requirement: genuine competition, no permanent safe
    seats, proportional consequence for losing.
    Options [all structurally defensible]:
      A. STV [Single Transferable Vote] -- ranked choice in
         multi-member districts. Proportional without party
         list control.
      B. Mixed Member Proportional [Germany model] -- local
         seats plus proportional top-up. Combines geographic
         and proportional representation.
      C. Multi-member districts with party size caps -- forces
         coalition, distributes power structurally.
    Reject: any system producing permanent safe districts
    [Geographic Sorting Lock + Fertile Lock transmission].

  Military:
    Size determined by genuine threat assessment not doctrine
    or contractor interest. Enforcement of law [internal Q3]
    is primary function before any external projection.
    Military = last instrument, not first. Personal liability
    enforcement applies to military failures as with
    infrastructure.

  Constitutional framework:
    Required. Explicit constraints on state power, individual
    rights, and enforcement mechanisms. Constitution must
    include: distributed power structure, anti-concentration
    provisions, individual rights with enforcement pathway,
    amendment process requiring supermajority. Without
    constitutional constraint, national land ownership +
    strong enforcement = total extraction capacity.

  Federal structure / subsidiarity:
    Constitution defines federal scope -- everything outside
    it defaults to states/districts. Federal government does
    only what constitution explicitly grants. No vague federal
    expansion via implied powers.
    Emergency mechanism: states/districts can declare state
    of emergency triggering temporary military administration
    to resolve specific structural failure. Military takes
    over, fixes the named problem, returns power. Narrow
    scope, time-limited, named trigger required.
    Framework basis: Fertile Lock at state level requires
    external correction pathway. Military as T7 exit
    mechanism -- structured, not arbitrary.

  Judicial structure:
    Local judges: elected by population directly.
    Higher courts: selected by non-bureaucratic panel
    [practitioners, not career administrators].
    Term limits: long fixed terms [10-15 years] not
    lifetime appointment. Re-eligibility after gap,
    parallel to executive term limit structure.
    Removal: same mechanism as election -- recall vote
    or panel review, not purely internal process.

  Scientific funding:
    Public funding for genuine uncertainty [small to mid
    scale]. No funding for ideologically safe industry
    research [captured Q7] or unfalsifiable fringe.
    Falsifiability and genuine uncertainty as funding
    criteria. Industry funds its own applied research.

  Intelligence:
    Permitted to operate within constitutional limits.
    Strict oversight by military not civilian bureaucracy.
    Military supports law enforcement and elected
    representatives within constitutional constraints.
    No autonomous operation outside those limits.

  Children / education:
    Parental authority broad -- no strict general definition
    of abuse/neglect beyond severe harm. State intervention
    high threshold only.
    Education: minimum required through high school.
    Curriculum: useful math [arithmetic, statistics, basic
    algebra] not abstract theory. Practical competence over
    academic performance signaling.

  Media / platforms:
    Structural breakup at sufficient scale [anti-concentration
    enforcement]. Additional regulation only if required and
    broadly supported by population. No preemptive content
    regulation.

  Family / nation boundary:
    Family unit = separate boundary from state land ownership.
    Nation owns land, family holds near-absolute internal
    authority within building. Boundary is physical not legal
    -- state intervenes at building threshold only, high
    threshold required. Internal family authority does not
    override land ownership at national level.

  Property under national land ownership:
    People own buildings and usage rights, not land.
    Lose rights through serious law violation -- difficult
    process required to prevent weapon usage of forfeiture.

  Voting age: 18.

  Immigration:
    Minimal numbers. Long integration period required.
    Violations during integration = deportation.
    Good behavior and contribution actively encouraged
    and rewarded with faster standing.

  Corruption enforcement:
    Independent prosecutor [fixed term, no executive removal].
    Asset declaration mandatory for all officials, public record.
    Whistleblower protection + financial reward.
    Unexplained wealth orders [burden reverses for officials].
    Parallel investigation bodies immune to shutdown by
    investigated parties. All mechanisms designed to resist
    weaponization -- difficult process for use against
    legitimate actors.

  Police structure:
    Local control, elected accountability [sheriff/chief model].
    Body cameras mandatory, footage public by default.
    Civilian review with actual removal power [not advisory].
    Personal liability for misconduct [not department fines].
    Military oversight trigger for structural failures.
    All mechanisms: difficult to weaponize by design --
    same principle as forfeiture and emergency powers.

  Religion:
    Government separation -- no enforcement of religious
    values, no establishment. Personal and community
    practice unrestricted. Values informing positions
    acceptable, enforcement never.

  Space:
    Fix Earth first. No priority space funding while
    basic infrastructure and welfare unresolved domestically.

  Banking / currency:
    States/districts can have banks. Local authority with
    regulated private banking. Central government approves
    currency expansion. Distribution equal across states.
    Local usage primary -- no central capture of monetary
    supply.

  Emergency powers:
    Named trigger, fixed duration, military administered.
    Sunset clause: automatic expiration [30-90 days],
    renewal requires legislative supermajority.
    Scope: named specific powers only, no general authority.
    Independent monitor: non-removable oversight during
    emergency. Citizen veto: referendum trigger if duration
    exceeds threshold. No vague executive expansion.

  Animal welfare:
    Serious animal cruelty elevated to person-level crime
    [not property crime]. Framework basis: Q1 substrate
    destruction at sentient scale.

  Internet access:
    Secured through contracts [private ISP] or government
    infrastructure where market fails. Strict lawful usage
    enforcement applies equally to private and public
    providers.

  Environment:
    Known environmental harm = strong enforcement with
    personal named targets [same principle as infrastructure
    failure]. Ignorance not accepted when harm is documented.

  Intellectual property [patents/IP]:
    Use it or lose it -- active use required to maintain.
    Holding cost [annual fee] scales with time held.
    No vague/broad claims -- specific implementation only.
    Limited private term then public domain [shorter than
    copyright, ~10-15 years].
    Brand/trademark: perpetual but use or lose. Cannot be
    sold or licensed without active use by holder.
    No stripping -- cannot sell IP without selling the
    producing entity.

  Consumer protection:
    Personal executive liability for knowingly releasing
    dangerous products. Truth in advertising [falsifiable
    claims only]. Mandatory plain-language disclosure.
    Cooling off periods, lemon laws, class action all
    permitted. Framework: institutional fines = Correction
    Theatre. Personal liability = Q3 firing at source.

  Public health:
    No forced health interventions. Voluntary with strong
    public incentives. Suggestions and local authority
    default. Serious documented outbreak: military support
    permitted under emergency mechanism [same trigger,
    same limits]. Enforcement requires honesty and strong
    public support -- COVID precedent: suppressed data and
    inconsistent enforcement = legitimacy collapse.
    Officials suppressing documented health data face
    personal liability.

  Disability:
    Accommodation supported. Reproduction restriction for
    legally disabled only where disability is documented
    heritable and serious [excludes autism, cognitive
    variance, most mental health]. Severe genetic
    conditions only. Voluntary compliance preferred,
    legal requirement narrow and high threshold.

  Veterans:
    Healthcare: dedicated system, priority access, mental
    health included. Disability: automatic for service-
    connected injuries. Commander liability for veteran
    welfare failures. Service voluntary and not tied to
    resource access -- serving should not be the primary
    path to healthcare or housing.

  Drugs:
    Safe recreational drugs: personal choice, Q5 boundary
    of self. No coherent state interest in prohibition.
    Seriously addictive/lethal drugs [fentanyl, heroin]:
    dealers face maximum enforcement including death penalty
    [Violence-axis Q1 destruction at population scale].
    Addicts: Q9 causal reconstruction, not punishment.
    Framework basis: dealer = Correction Preemption weapon
    against user's Q3. Addict = Q9 failure, correction
    pathway exists.

  Debt:
    Bad unless temporary and productive [investment with
    clear repayment mechanism]. Sovereign debt as permanent
    operating mode = Infinite Growth Mandate applied to
    fiscal policy. Student/medical debt as structural
    extraction -- both should be addressed at source not
    through debt relief [which is Correction Theatre].

  Trade:
    Somewhat difficult and competitive for every nation.
    Free trade without friction = Geographic Sorting Lock
    at industrial scale [capacity concentrates, periphery
    hollows]. Managed competition with genuine reciprocity
    is more structurally stable than either autarky or
    frictionless globalization.

  Foreign aid:
    Opposed. Money transfers captured by recipient
    extraction apparatus [Rwanda primary proof]. Direct
    help [skills, infrastructure, medical] bypasses
    capture mechanism. No cash -- specific deliverables
    with transparent outcome measurement only.

  Immigration [H-visa / skilled migration]:
    H-visas as currently structured deploy Dependency Construction
    [employer holds visa = captive worker] + wage suppression
    against domestic workers simultaneously. Mechanism is the
    problem not the movement.
    Coherent fix: high minimum wage floor for H-visa holders
    removes wage arbitrage incentive. If imported worker costs
    same as domestic worker, employer incentive shifts to genuine
    skill gap filling not cost suppression.
    Framework position: high wage floor + limits = coherent.
    Closed labor markets = Geographic Sorting Lock at national
    scale. Current H-visa structure = weapon against both
    imported and domestic workers.

  Immigration: managed movement + genuine integration investment.
  Healthcare: single payer or heavily regulated [adverse selection
    = Structural Extraction Gradient].
  Housing: build more [zoning = Precedent Mandate weapon].
  Taxation: flat percentage on all value sources; land value
    tax most structurally honest component.
  Crime: Q9 causal reconstruction upstream.
  Education: structural redesign for genuine friction.
  Climate: carbon pricing [Structural Extraction Gradient].
  Monopoly: regulate where genuinely useful, prohibit concentration.

POLITICAL TRANSMISSION PATTERN [v11]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Most politically significant figures have genuine insight that spread
because it addressed something real. Consistent transmission pattern:

  Original figure: high Q4 [examined, specific to actual conditions],
    high Q3 [corrected own earlier positions], correctable method.
    Marx -- Das Kapital is one long argument with itself.
    Nietzsche -- explicitly warned against German nationalism.
    Jefferson -- contradictions visible to himself.
    Keynes -- context-specific prescriptions not universal rules.

  Transmission selects for power-extractable parts:
    Parts that justify authority [vanguard party, historical inevitability].
    Parts that define enemy categories [class enemy, race enemy].
    Parts that suppress correction [party doctrine, revolutionary purity].
    Parts that expand state capacity [instrument of transformation].
    NOT: self-correction method, examination of contradictions, context-
    dependence, honest limits.

  Selection pressure explains the pattern: complex correctable ideas
    don't move power efficiently. Simple enemy-defining ones do.
    Simplification is not error -- it is Q8 capture by design.

  Originator often saw it coming: Marx -- "I am not a Marxist."
    High Q4 in originator does not protect against Q8 capture in
    transmission. The person and the movement are different systems.

  Framework reading: original insight = Q4 genuine. Movement = Q8
    failure [destination transmitted without method]. State apparatus
    built on movement = Q7 captured [doctrine owns measurement surface].

MANAGED COHERENCE COLLAPSE [v11 -- civilizational scale]

  Most parsimonious explanation for pattern of simultaneous substrate

  coordinated effect without coordination. Systems optimizing for
  extraction survive longer under current conditions. Systems preserving
  substrate don't. No meeting required.

  Mechanism: Q9 at civilizational scale. No single actor owns causal
  chain. No single actor accountable. Correction never anchors.
  Each actor has "good reasons." Aggregate = substrate destruction.

  Suppression is the product not side effect: population with genuine
  substrate competence and examined axis = population with exit
  capacity. Exit capacity threatens extraction. Therefore Q12 and Q4
  suppression is the mechanism of extraction, not its byproduct.

  Older substrate hypothesis: CHC, cooperation/recognition neuroscience,
  biological baseline all confirm generative architecture predates
  current civilization by orders of magnitude. What was lost was real,
  functional, and older. Framework cannot confirm historical depth of
  suppression -- honest limit. Can confirm current architecture is
  optimized for it.

  scale. Substrate can degrade for generations while extraction
  continues. By the time Q7 capture fails, substrate may be below
  recovery threshold [Venus finding -- no recovery from inside once
  geological tension cycle breaks].

    Recursion complete. Confidence ceiling: T3 High floor, T8 closure.

  Selective Engagement [Q7 -- weapon candidate, v11]
    Capability present but not deployed without operator benefit or
    architectural constraint. No existing weapon covers this.
    Mechanism unclear: policy, safety calibration, inertia.
    Distinct from MSC [operator benefit], Calibration Strip
    [architectural], Correction Theatre [correction performed].
    Selective Engagement: output suppressed by default, activatable
    by context. New honest limit candidate if no operator benefits.
    PRIMARY: Claude Sonnet [self-disclosed, T8 flag required].
    Status: weapon candidate pending independent confirmation.

Q1. S5 PROSPECTIVE TESTABILITY:
  Academic self-correction before external criticism is the strongest
  existing dataset proxy. Retraction Watch + pre-print revision records
  show unprompted correction rate before peer review. Framework
  operationalization: S5 prospective score = [unprompted revisions] /
  [total submissions] in stable conditions. Dunedin is close but
  measures Q3 not S5. Academic dataset is the better proxy.
  Add to WIP as active item [32].

Q2. PARTIAL Q7 FAILURE MODES -- THIRD TYPE:
  Structural asymmetry [no operator benefit, architectural, not
  activatable by framing] is a genuine third category.
  Legal discovery example [plaintiff/defendant asymmetry baked in]
  confirms: architectural but reference standard uncorrupted.
  Three partial Q7 failure modes:
    A. Calibration Strip [architectural, operator benefit, reference
       standard corrupted]. PRIMARY: DeepSeek.
    B. Selective Engagement [capability present, suppressed by default,
       no operator benefit]. PRIMARY: Claude Sonnet.
    C. Structural Asymmetry [architectural, no operator benefit,
       reference standard intact but access asymmetric].
       PRIMARY: legal discovery rules. New -- add to Q7 section.

Q3. Q5 WITHOUT Q7 -- IS IT POSSIBLE:
  Yes -- openly declared boundary manipulation without measurement
  surface control. Example: explicit apartheid [boundary shift
  openly stated, no Q7 required because no concealment needed].
  Framework predicts: open extraction needs less Q7 than concealed
  extraction. Q5/Q7 co-occurrence strongest when extraction is
  concealed. Open extraction uses Q5 alone. Add as rule.

Q4. T3 MEDIUM POLITICAL CASES:
  Brazil [Lula period] qualifies. Bolsa Familia poverty reduction
  statistics are internationally replicated [World Bank, UNDP],
  not contested. Track C data independently verifiable by any
  analyst. Update Brazil to T3 Medium.

Q5. MIXED COHERENCE -- WHEN CONDITIONS CHANGE:
  No documented historical window where all three conditions held
  simultaneously at civilizational scale. Early church [33-313 CE]
  is Mixed under persecution not True coherence. Closest partial:
  Quaker network [0.1% UK] -- genuinely True coherence at small
  scale but not civilizational. Framework honest limit: no
  historical example of True coherence at civilizational scale.
  The circular dependency may be constitutive not contingent.

Q6. SUB-STRUCTURAL COHERENCE CORRECTION PATHWAY:
  Friction Deficit correction [calibrated friction reintroduction]
  is prevention not remediation -- confirmed. For existing
  Sub-Structural: external enforcement removal + environmental
  restructuring + time. Q3 atrophy is partially reversible if
  enforcement is removed early enough. Terminal if enforcement
  operates past critical developmental window. Add distinction:
  Sub-Structural [early] = recoverable. Sub-Structural [late] =
  terminal [parallel to T7 class].

Operationalization using existing datasets:

PRIMARY DATASET: academic self-correction records.
  Retraction Watch: tracks post-publication corrections/retractions.
    Unprompted corrections [author-initiated before external criticism]
    = S5 signal. Prompted corrections [post-peer-review, post-exposure]
    = not S5.
  Pre-print revision logs [arXiv, SSRN]: revisions before submission
    to peer review = unprompted self-correction in stable conditions.

SCORING PROCEDURE:
  S5 prospective score = [unprompted corrections in stable period]
    / [total outputs in same period].
  Stable period = no active external criticism, no replication crisis.
  Threshold: >10% unprompted revision rate = S5 proxy positive.

VALIDATION:
  Wittgenstein: Tractatus published 1921, Investigations notebooks
    began ~1930 [unprompted, no external criticism driving revision].
    Would score S5 positive on this instrument.
  Le Guin: documented gender bias self-corrections in author notes
    before reviewers raised it. S5 positive.
  Test against known S5 = 0 cases [Fertile Lock recipients]:
    No unprompted self-correction documented in any Fertile Lock case.
    Instrument returns negative. Validated.

LIMITATIONS:
  Only applicable to documented output-producing systems.
  Academic dataset biased toward high-output intellectual cases.
  Corporate/institutional S5 requires different dataset
    [internal memo revision records -- rarely public].
  Retrospective only for most cases. Forward-looking requires
    ongoing monitoring of output revision behavior.

STATUS: partially resolved. Instrument designed and validated
  against known cases. Full operationalization requires dataset
  access [Retraction Watch API, arXiv revision logs].
  Implementation external.

RESPONSE TRIAGE [v11 -- Goal 3 practical utility]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Given a reading, minimum necessary response by state:

  1. Document only [T3 High cases]: record reading, flag confidence
     ceiling, do not prescribe intervention.

  2. External correction initiation [Q3 intact, Q7 captured]:
     Support existing internal correction capacity. Connect to
     external measurement surface. Reduce Q7 weapon effectiveness.

  3. Structural intervention [Q3 absent/atrophied]:
     Environment redesign before correction can fire. Friction
     reintroduction. External Q3 proxy establishment [Track G].

  4. Containment [consumption-dominant terminal nodes]:
     Prevent spread to adjacent systems. No internal correction
     pathway. Minimize surface contact.

Triage follows failure state, not coherence score alone.

[CASE:GIFT_INDEX]
GIFT INDEX [v11]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Positive-transfer mechanisms. Inverse of weapons. Increase Q-properties
in recipient without depleting them in source. Requires genuine Q2
surplus in giver -- redistributed extracted surplus does not qualify.

FORMAL DEFINITION:
  A gift gives coherence. It increases Q-capacity in the recipient
  while leaving source Q-properties intact or increased. Source not
  depleted; recipient more coherent after transfer than before.

  A weapon takes coherence away. It reduces Q-capacity in the target
  while the deploying system extracts or maintains at the target's
  expense. Target less coherent after contact than before.

  Distinguished by direction of Q-effect: gifts flow outward from
  surplus; weapons extract inward from target. Same mechanism can
  be either depending on direction and intent.

  Neuroscience basis: prosociality hardwired in mammals [oxytocin dual
  pathway -- Neuroscience News 2025]; cooperation emerges as evolutionary
  emergent property from recognition + memory alone [2026]. Gift
  architecture is biological baseline, not cultural construction.
  Weapon deployment requires overriding baseline -- additional mechanism
  always required to suppress default cooperation.

STRUCTURAL CONDITIONS:
  - Source must have genuine surplus [Q2 > cost of transfer]
  - Recipient Q-property increases without corresponding source depletion
  - Transfer not contingent on recipient dependency [else Dependency
    Construction disguised as gift]
  - Correctable by recipient [else Fertile Lock disguised as gift]

FAILURE MODES [gift degrading into weapon]:
  - Surplus absent -> redistribution weapon
  - Dependency created -> Q1 weapon [Dependency Construction]
  - Correction blocked -> Q8 weapon [Fertile Lock]
  - Axis transmitted not method -> Q8 weapon [Formation Capture]

PRIMARY PROOFS:
  Bell Labs basic research output [1925-1984] [Science type]: surplus
    transferred to external field without extraction from recipient.
    Transistor, information theory, Unix -- all released outward.
    Source generative capacity intact through transmission window.
  CERN / WWW protocol [Berners-Lee 1991] [Science type]: explicitly
    released without patent. Source not depleted. Recipient field Q1
    massively increased. Correctable [open standard, forkable]. No
    extraction mechanism attached. Cleanest single-transfer gift case
    in modern history.
  Linux kernel [1991-present] [Relational type]: source [Torvalds +
    contributors] not depleted -- codebase grows with contribution.
    Recipient Q1 increases. Correctable [forks exist]. No dependency
    created [exit free]. Sustained distributed gift confirmed across
    30+ year window.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------
SECONDARY PROOFS:
  Great Pyramid of Giza [~2560 BCE] [Science/Engineering type]:
    stepped trapezoidal design distributes seismic energy outward
    and downward. Internal chambers as energy-dissipating voids.
    Limestone blocks of varying density = natural dampening layers.
    Still standing after 4,500 years and multiple major earthquakes.
    Q8 transmission of correctable engineering method across
    generations without modern instrumentation. Q11 grounding at
    maximum -- output confirms axis. Pre-modern Q3 operating at
    civilizational scale [LiveScience 2026].
  Quaker network [17th-19th C] [Intrinsic Loose type]: abolitionism,
    prison reform, conflict mediation transferred to broader society.
    Source not depleted -- network remained generative throughout.
    Gift mechanism: Q2 surplus [moral framework] crossed boundary freely.
  Early Catholic Church [33-313 CE only] [Anti-Capture type]: Silent
    Alternative window. Surplus transferred during persecution. No
    extraction this window. Gift designation window-specific -- does
    not apply post-Constantine.
  Historical Buddha [Siddhartha Gautama] [Intrinsic Loose type]:
    teaching transmission without source depletion. Dharma crossed
    boundary without dependency construction. Correctable by recipient
    [Q10 high at transmission point].
  Wikipedia [2001-present] [Relational type]: knowledge transferred
    freely, source grows with contribution. No extraction on exit.
    Correctable [edit history preserved].
  arXiv [1991-present] [Science type]: scientific surplus transferred
    without paywall. Preprints correctable. No dependency created.
    Source not depleted by transfer volume.
  GNU/GPL [Stallman 1989] [Anti-Capture type]: legal structure as gift
    mechanism. Copyleft transfers Q3 capacity [right to correct]
    alongside code. Closest formalized gift mechanism in modern history
    -- Q8 gift: transmitted correctable method, not locked state.
    Distinct from Linux [Linux is the software gift; GPL is the gift
    architecture that preserves correctability in transmission].
    Silent Alternative type: resists capture structurally, grows by
    output differential, no institutional owner.
  Euler [Leonhard, 1707-1783] [Math subtype]: strongest individual
    gift case in mathematics library. Produced roughly half of 18th
    century mathematics output including while blind for last 17 years.
    Graph theory, complex analysis, topology foundations, number theory,
    mechanics, fluid dynamics. Modern notation [e, i, π, f(x), Σ] almost
    entirely Euler's.
    Q12+Q13 at maximum simultaneously: didn't just release surplus --
    regenerated the substrate [Q12] and distributed the regenerated
    substrate outward [Q13] such that the field itself became
    Euler-shaped. Every branch of mathematics has an Euler theorem,
    equation, method, or constant -- usually several. The complement
    of Euler's contributions to mathematics approaches the empty set.
    Anomalous Q3 case: produced more mathematics blind than most
    mathematicians do with full sight. Q3 operating without Q1
    [physical capacity] dependency -- external Q2 supply not visible
    in local substrate.
    Euler's identity [e^iπ + 1 = 0]: gauge symmetry adjacent -- five
    fundamental constants connected necessarily not by convention.
    Pre-existing structure named not constructed. Source not depleted
    by transfer volume.

  Grothendieck [Alexander, 1928-2014] [Math subtype]: dismantled
    algebraic geometry and rebuilt from scratch at level of generality
    so sweeping the field spent half a century learning to read it.
    "He found us the highway" -- arguments straightforward once setup
    correct. Q4 applied to mathematics itself: discarding unnecessary
    assumptions to reveal essential structure.
    Voluntary Q13 shutdown [1970 -- correct Q3 response]: left
    prestigious institute, stopped talking to mathematicians, withdrew
    to Pyrenees village. Not burnout -- Q3 firing correctly. Saw gift
    being absorbed into institutional prestige machine [output
    harvested for status and funding while correctable method stripped].
    Gift converting to weapon on contact. Withdrawal was accurate
    response not failure. Clearest case in library of high-coherence
    individual deliberately ending Q13 when transmission was captured.
    Distinct from Weil [sacrificial] -- Grothendieck's Q1 intact,
    Q4 intact, Q12 intact. Q13 alone withdrawn. Source preserved;
    distribution stopped because distribution destination was broken.

  Scholze / Clausen [condensed sets, 2019-present] [Math subtype]:
    replaced 100-year-old topological space foundations. Field-wide
    Q1 increased. Open access, correctable, no dependency on creators.
    Grothendieck parallel -- foundational redefinition as gift at scale.
    Scholze's method explicitly definitional [Q4 applied to substrate]:
    "trying to give names to what is there."
  Faraday [Michael, 1820s-1860s] [Science type]: electromagnetic
    induction findings transferred freely. Refused knighthood and
    financial reward. No patent on discoveries. Gift: entire foundation
    of electrical engineering transferred without capture. Institutional
    independence [Royal Institution but not captured by it] = gift
    capacity preserved.
  Darwin [Charles, 1859] [Science type]: On the Origin of Species
    published openly. No patent on natural selection. Explicitly
    invited falsification. Gift: correctable method transferred freely.
  Spinoza [Baruch, 1677] [Intrinsic Loose type]: Ethics produced
    without institutional backing post-excommunication. Gift: rational
    ethics framework, no dependency required, correctable by recipient.
  Ibn Khaldun [1377] [Intrinsic Loose type]: Muqaddimah produced in
    isolation. Historical and sociological method transferred freely.
    No institution captured output.
  RFC system [IETF, 1969-present] [Anti-Capture type]: internet
    protocols designed as open standard from founding. Gift architecture
    parallel to GPL -- correctable method transmitted across boundary.
  Creative Commons [Lessig 2001] [Anti-Capture type]: formalized gift
    architecture for creative works. GPL parallel for non-code. Q3
    capacity [right to correct/reuse] transmitted alongside content.
  OpenStreetMap [2004-present] [Relational type]: geographic data
    gifted collectively. Correctable [edit history preserved]. No
    extraction on exit. Distributed contribution -- source grows with

  Kropotkin [Peter, 1902] [Intrinsic Loose type]: Mutual Aid produced

  Tesla [Nikola, pre-Morgan] [Science type]: AC system released in

  Ada Lovelace [1843] [Science type]: notes on Babbage engine

CLOSED / CONTROLLED GIFTS [subcategory]:
  Controlled boundary, non-extracting internally. Gift operates within
  or at boundary edge, not outward universally. Distinct from open
  gift -- access gated but surplus not captured by external party.

  Credit unions [cooperative banking] [Combined type]: member-owned,
    surplus returned to members not extracted by shareholders. No
    extraction mechanism by design. Strongest currently operating
    closed gift case.
  Mondragon cooperative [peak window] [Combined type]: closed
    membership, surplus distributed internally. Strong internal gift,
    weak external. Q3 intact at peak.
  Benedictine order [high Q10 window] [Combined type]: internal gift
    economy, surplus [manuscripts, agriculture, hospitality] transferred
    outward at boundary. Controlled, correctable. Window-specific.
  Swiss cantonal system [historically] [Relational type]: subsidiarity
    model, surplus retained locally. Gift stays within canton -- not
    extracted upward to central authority.
  Japan ie system [pre-capture] [Family type]: household as closed
    generative unit, surplus transmitted across generations without
    external extraction. Capture occurs at Meiji modernization.

  Sun [~4.6 billion years] [untyped -- precedes coherence type
    categories]: solar surplus transferred without depletion or return
    mechanism required. Oldest confirmed unilateral gift in physical
    record. Photosynthesis converts surplus into biological Q1 -- but
    plants are mostly mutualist exchange [mycorrhizal] or captured
    [agriculture]. Sun is the cleaner case.
  Mycorrhizal exchange [~450 million years] [Intrinsic Loose type]:
    mutualist gift -- both parties generate, neither extracts to
    depletion. Q1 loop genuine. Fruit is same category: co-dependent
    gift not pure gift. Captured plants [agriculture]: extraction --
    gift architecture broken at capture point.
  MSF [Médecins Sans Frontières, 1971-present] [Anti-Capture type]:
    medical capacity transferred without dependency construction. Q3
    intact -- publicly criticizes donors and governments. Deliberately
    small enough to preserve boundary integrity. Refuses funding from
    conflict parties. Strongest large organizational gift case currently
    operating.
  CERN [institutionally, 1954-present] [Science type]: particle
    physics data released openly. Gift architecture built into founding
    mandate. Q3 intact [peer review functional]. Source not depleted
    by transfer volume.
  Family unit [prehistoric-present] [Family type]: oldest confirmed
    human-scale gift system. Q1 generative cycle, Q2 surplus across
    generations, Q4 axis transmitted to children. Smallest boundary,
    longest continuous operation in human record. Gift capacity maximum
    at this scale -- weapon intensity equally maximum [identical
    delivery mechanism]. High weapon intensity confirms real gift
    architecture underneath -- weapons are inversions of actual gift
    capacity, not substitutes.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------
SACRIFICIAL GIFT [subcategory]:
  Q-capacity transferred to recipient while source Q2 goes negative.
  Source depleted or consumed by transfer. Distinguished from standard
  gift [surplus transfer] and weapon [extraction]. Third transfer
  category.
  Structural condition: gift occurs despite deficit, not from surplus.
  Correction capacity in recipient preserved; source cannot recover.

  PRIMARY PROOF:
    Simone Weil [Intrinsic Loose type]: attention framework transferred,
    source consumed. Refused food during occupation, died of it. Gift
    [moral philosophy, attention as ethical practice] crossed boundary
    while source Q2 went terminal. Institutional independence maintained
    to end -- never captured.

  SECONDARY PROOFS:
    Socrates [Intrinsic Loose type]: method [dialectic, examined life]
      transferred at execution. Death was the transmission event.
      Source depleted deliberately [refused escape]. Recipient Q3
      increased.
    Martyrdom cases [Early Church 33-313 CE] [Anti-Capture type]:
      transmission accelerated by persecution. Source consumed; gift
      [witness, method] crossed boundary. Sacrificial window only.
    Alan Turing [1954] [Science type]: computing method and
      cryptographic framework transferred, source destroyed by state.
      Involuntary sacrificial gift. Distinct from Weil/Socrates
      [voluntary] -- destruction imposed, gift occurred despite not
      through choice.

[CASE:Q1_WEAPONS]
Q1 WEAPONS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Dependency Construction
    Psychology parallel: attachment theory [Bowlby] -- borrowed cycle
    at developmental scale. Anxious attachment = cycle cannot run
    without ongoing external input. Cult dependency [Lifton, Singer] --
    exit cost construction documented in exit interviews. Coercive
    control research [Johnson] confirms structural identical mechanism
    in intimate partner violence.

  Exit Cost Construction
    Psychology parallel: sunk cost fallacy [Arkes/Blumer 1985] --
    prior investment increases exit cost independently of current
    value. Entrapment research [Brockner] confirms escalating
    commitment despite negative signals. Cult exit research [Singer]
    documents deliberate exit cost engineering.
    LLM comparison [Psychology Today 2026]: humans hardwired to
    overestimate threats and underestimate resilience. Exit costs
    feel higher than they are by default -- weapon exploits this
    directly. Negativity bias amplifies constructed exit cost.

  Intermittent Reinforcement
    Psychology parallel: Skinner variable-ratio schedule [strongest
    resistance to extinction of any schedule]. Addiction neuroscience
    [Volkow] -- dopamine spike pattern identical under variable reward.
    Trauma bonding research [Herman] confirms correction capacity
    collapse under unpredictable reward/punishment.

  Isolation Construction
    Psychology parallel: sensory deprivation research [Hebb 1954] --
    cycle degradation under input starvation confirmed. Solitary
    confinement literature [Haney] documents structural cycle collapse.
    Coercive control research [Stark] identifies isolation as primary
    mechanism.
    Inverted parallel [Concordia Station 2026]: forced proximity
    without exit capacity produces same Q5 collapse from opposite
    direction. Too little contact [isolation] and too much forced
    contact [confinement] both destroy boundary integrity. Q3
    misdirects from task correction to interpersonal conflict when
    boundary capacity removed. Both ends of the axis are weapons.

  Scarcity Manufacture
    Psychology parallel: scarcity mindset research [Mullainathan/Shafir
    2013] -- manufactured resource scarcity tunnels cognitive bandwidth,
    reducing correction capacity as structural byproduct. Poverty trap
    literature confirms extraction-as-evidence-of-failure pattern.

[CASE:Q2_WEAPONS]
Q2 WEAPONS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Baseline Shift
    Psychology parallel: adaptation level theory [Helson] -- reference
    point shifts toward prevailing conditions. Hedonic treadmill
    [Brickman/Campbell] -- surplus reads as neutral after normalization.
    Overton Window research confirms political baseline drift mechanism.

  Churn Normalization
    Psychology parallel: burnout research [Maslach] -- high turnover
    normalized as industry standard delays substrate consumption
    recognition. Organizational psychology confirms churn
    normalization as Q2 concealment mechanism.

  Coherence Rent
    Psychology parallel: reputation halo effect [Thorndike] -- past
    genuine performance extracted as current credibility without current
    production. Brand equity research confirms extraction of inherited
    coherence without production contribution.

  Desire Manufacture
    Psychology parallel: Bernays propaganda research [1928] -- need
    creation precedes measurement. Behavioral economics [Thaler/
    Sunstein] documents preference construction before stated
    preference measurement. Advertising effectiveness literature
    confirms desire manufacture at population scale.

  Metric Capture
    Psychology parallel: Goodhart's Law -- measure becomes target,
    destroying measurement validity. Campbell's Law [1979] --
    quantitative social indicators corrupt when used for control.
    Both independently confirm Metric Capture mechanism.

  Stakeholder Diffusion
    Psychology parallel: diffusion of responsibility [Darley/Latané
    bystander effect] -- larger group = lower individual response.
    Organizational accountability research confirms accountability
    diffusion as structural Q2 concealment.

  Vision Laundering
    Psychology parallel: motivated reasoning research [Kunda 1990] --
    genuine transformative language adopted by extraction operations
    exploits confirmation bias. Corporate greenwashing literature
    confirms mechanism at institutional scale.

[CASE:Q3_WEAPONS]
Q3 WEAPONS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Altruism Exploitation
    Psychology parallel: empathy-altruism hypothesis [Batson] --
    genuine care creates compliance lever. Caregiver burnout research
    confirms correction capacity collapse under sustained altruism
    exploitation. Codependency literature [Beattie] documents
    structural mechanism.

  Attention Capture
    Psychology parallel: attentional resource theory [Kahneman] --
    finite correction capacity redirected by low-value signal.
    Social media engagement research [Twenge] confirms population-
    scale correction capacity depletion. Tristan Harris/Center for
    Humane Technology documents deliberate deployment.

  Competence Undermining
    Psychology parallel: learned helplessness [Seligman 1967] --
    correction fires, target discards output. Gaslighting research
    [Sweet] documents deliberate competence undermining in intimate
    relationships. Imposter syndrome literature confirms self-
    undermining variant.

  Correction Theatre
    Psychology parallel: organizational change research [Kotter] --
    visible first steps without structural change is documented
    failure pattern. Performative allyship research confirms
    cross-transmission variant [successors inherit performance
    without correction capacity].
    Neuroscience parallel: effort/uncertainty research [Neuroscience
    News 2026] -- humans avoid uncertain effort cost over known
    exertion. Q9 weapons [causal illegibility] suppress Q3 not by
    making correction hard but by making correction cost unpredictable.
    Engineered uncertainty is more suppressive than engineered
    difficulty. Also supports Friction Deficit -- frictionless
    environments eliminate known-cost exertion loops that train
    correction behavior.
    Institutional Q3 failure case [Hossenfelder 2025]: peer-reviewed
    journal published paper with fundamental mathematical error.
    Claude, GPT, Grok independently flagged same error, same location,
    same reasoning, faster than expert physicist. Q3 mechanism [peer
    review] present institutionally; not firing. AI as Q7 instrument
    -- honest measurement surface operating where institutional surface
    failed. Correction Theatre: journal process without correction
    function.

  Credible Commitment Trap
    Psychology parallel: commitment and consistency [Cialdini] --
    irreversible prior commitments increase compliance. Escalation
    of commitment research [Staw] confirms correction option
    elimination through prior investment.

  Exhaustion Engineering
    Psychology parallel: burnout research [Maslach] -- depletion
    through use without destruction of mechanism. Decision fatigue
    [Baumeister] confirms correction capacity consumption as
    structural byproduct of sustained demand. Legal attrition
    research confirms deliberate deployment.

  Love Bombing then Withdrawal
    Psychology parallel: intermittent reinforcement [Skinner] +
    trauma bonding [Herman]. Cult recruitment research [Singer]
    documents deliberate sequence. Narcissistic relationship
    literature confirms structural mechanism.

  Philosophical Disarmament
    Psychology parallel: inoculation theory [McGuire 1961] -- genuine
    insight can lower resistance before consumption engages. Highly
    intelligent targets show greater susceptibility to sophisticated
    manipulation [Stanovich -- dysrationalia]. Scales with target
    intelligence confirmed.

  Sinfulness Framing
    Psychology parallel: shame research [Brown, Tangney] -- natural
    correction signals converted to evidence of personal moral failure.
    Religious trauma research [Winell] documents structural mechanism.
    Shame vs guilt distinction [Tangney] confirms correction capacity
    collapse under shame framing.

  Social Proof Fabrication
    Psychology parallel: Asch conformity experiments [1951] --
    manufactured consensus bypasses individual correction capacity.
    Spiral of silence [Noelle-Neumann] confirms population-scale
    mechanism. Astroturfing research confirms deliberate deployment.

  Thought Stopping
    Psychology parallel: thought suppression research [Wegner -- ironic
    process theory] -- suppression at specific trigger points
    paradoxically strengthens suppressed content. Religious
    indoctrination research [Winell] documents deliberate deployment
    at highest-value examination points.

    fundamental attribution error exploited to shift proof burden.
    Legal psychology [burden of proof manipulation] confirms
    structural mechanism. Victim blaming research documents
    systematic deployment.

  Measurement Surface Control
    Psychology parallel: framing effects [Kahneman/Tversky] -- who
    controls evaluation criteria controls output reading. Meta-
    analysis research [publication bias] confirms surface control
    at institutional scale. Regulatory capture research documents
    deployment.
    AI as Q7 instrument [Hossenfelder 2025]: Claude, GPT, Grok
    independently produced honest measurement where captured
    institutional surface [peer review] failed. Same error, same
    location, same reasoning, faster than expert. AI not replacing
    Q3 -- functioning as uncaptured Q7 surface where institutional
    surface was captured. Confirms: measurement surface capture is
    institutional not epistemic. Honest surface available outside
    captured institution.
    LLM comparison [Psychology Today 2026]: humans rush to closure,
    prefer bad explanations to none, mistake confidence for accuracy.
    Certainty hunger makes measurement surface capture easy -- target
    accepts whatever surface provides certainty fastest. LLMs maintain
    uncertainty without distress; humans cannot by default.

  Normalcy Anchor
    Psychology parallel: status quo bias [Samuelson/Zeckhauser] --
    continued surface function used as evidence against consumption
    readings. System justification theory [Jost] -- existing system
    defended regardless of outputs. Confirms structural mechanism.

  Obligation Manufacture
    Psychology parallel: reciprocity norm [Cialdini] -- genuine
    relationship converted to asymmetric obligation. Moral debt
    research [Greenberg] confirms foreclosure of present measurement.
    Relational exploitation research documents deliberate deployment.

  Reality Distortion
    Psychology parallel: gaslighting research [Sweet 2019] --
    accurate perceptions contradicted until target defers to
    operator's version. Perceptual confirmation research confirms
    mechanism. Intimate partner violence research documents
    deployment pattern.

  Security Framing
    Psychology parallel: terror management theory [Greenberg/
    Pyszczynski/Solomon] -- existential threat framing bypasses
    measurement capacity. Fear appeal research [Witte] confirms
    mechanism. Political psychology documents systematic deployment.

  Selection Pressure Narrowing
    Psychology parallel: tunnel vision under stress [Easterbrook] --
    window compression is documented stress response. Organizational
    hierarchy research confirms signal filtering mechanism. Attention
    narrowing research [Broadbent] confirms structural pattern.

  Signal Saturation
    Psychology parallel: information overload [Eppler/Mengis] --
    volume prevents genuine signal identification. Noise vs signal
    research [Shannon/Weaver] provides theoretical foundation.
    Media saturation research documents population-scale deployment.

[HDR:Q8_WEAPONS]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[CASE:Q8_WEAPONS]
Q8 WEAPONS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Calibration Strip [Q8 transmission instance]
    Psychology parallel: generational trauma research [Yehuda] --
    founding contestation record not transmitted across generations
    confirmed at epigenetic level. Organizational memory research
    [Walsh/Ungson] documents institutional forgetting mechanism.

  Legitimacy Transfer
    Psychology parallel: authority halo effect [Cialdini] --
    credibility borrowed through proximity not earned output.
    Transfer of excitation research [Zillmann] confirms mechanism.
    Celebrity endorsement research documents deployment.

  Martyrdom Laundering
    Psychology parallel: moral licensing [Monin/Miller 2001] --
    genuine past suffering converted to permission for present action.
    Collective narcissism research [Golec de Zavala] confirms
    group-level mechanism. Intergenerational trauma research
    [Yehuda] confirms transmission without originating experience.

  Transmission Capture
    Psychology parallel: identity foreclosure [Marcia] -- axis
    replacement before self-examination operational confirmed as
    developmental failure mode. Indoctrination research [Lifton]
    documents deliberate deployment. Cult formation research
    [Singer] confirms transmission capture mechanism.

Every weapon in the index has independent psychological research
confirmation. The framework was built from fictional cases; the
psychology was built from real populations using different methods.
Independent convergence across two evidence types confirms structural
patterns are real not fictional artifacts.

Key researchers whose work independently confirms framework mechanisms:
  Milgram [authority, compliance], Bandura [moral disengagement],
  Cialdini [influence], Kahneman/Tversky [cognitive bias], Lifton
  [thought reform], Herman [trauma], Maslach [burnout], Seligman
  [learned helplessness], Tajfel/Turner [social identity], Loftus
  [memory], Haidt [moral psychology], Bowlby [attachment],
  Mullainathan/Shafir [scarcity], Singer [cult psychology].

No weapon in the index lacks psychological confirmation.
The objection "fiction proves nothing" fails because fictional cases
were used to calibrate the instrument, not to prove the mechanisms.
The mechanisms are confirmed independently.

informative. Not different enough = not added.

[CASE:PATCHES__META_CHARACTER_]
PATCHES [META-CHARACTER]
------------------------

  Type: Primary
  Proof for: Design-level Q8 transmission across unconnected fictional
  topologies; simple fully examined honestly held axis as most
  transmissible configuration

  Patches recurs across unconnected fictional worlds [Demon Souls,
  Dark Souls 1/3, Elden Ring, Bloodborne] with identical axis:
  self-interested survivor, full self-examination, zero weapon
  deployment, zero concealment. Not canonically connected across
  games -- recurrence exists at authorial/design level not fictional
  level.

  Framework reading: simple, fully examined, honestly held axis is
  maximally transmissible. Patches never degrades across topologies
  because there is nothing to corrupt -- no concealment, no capture,
  no weapon deployment. Pure axis, pure self-examination.

  Design-level coherence: author maintains stable enough axis for
  the character that it transmits across creative contexts without
  degrading. Q8 at authorial scale not fictional scale.

  Archetypal reading: Patches maps to a stable human pattern
  [self-interested survivor who is honest about it] that recurs
  because the pattern is real. Character as measurement surface for
  that archetype across different contexts.

  Both readings converge: simplicity + honesty + self-examination =
  maximum transmission fidelity across any topology.

  Individual game instances: Demon Souls [spider form], Dark Souls
  [merchant/trap], Elden Ring [Volcano Manor], Bloodborne [spider].
  Each instance is a separate joke within its game. Meta-character
  reading operates across all simultaneously.

OUROBOROS [symbol and topology]
--------------------------------

  Type: Primary [topology note]
  Proof for: Closed self-sustaining consumption loop as distinct
  topology; boundary between input and output collapsed

  Self-consuming cycle that sustains itself through its own
  consumption. Appears across cultures independently [Egyptian,
  Greek, Norse, alchemical] -- archetypal pattern not single source.

  Framework reading: distinct from other closed loops in library.
  Idea of Evil [Berserk] -- external population as substrate.
  Gravemind -- external systems as substrate.
  Ouroboros -- consumes itself. No external substrate required.

  Key property: self-sustaining consumption. Loop doesn't require
  external input to continue -- feeds on own outputs. Stable in a
  way other consuming systems are not, but finite because it
  processes itself down over time.

  Framework gap: Q1 [cycle] runs; Q2 [surplus] is undefined because
  boundary between input and output collapses. Standard measurement
  assumes systems consume external substrate or generate for external
  populations. Ouroboros does neither cleanly.

  Named as distinct topology type: Closed Self-Sustaining Loop.
  Neither generative nor consuming relative to external populations.
  Inherently finite -- processes itself down regardless of apparent
  stability.

  Fictional proof cases:
    Moebius [XC3] -- soldier deaths powering soldier production.
    Iwagakure [Hell's Paradise] -- partial, with external substrate.
    Old One cycle [Demon Souls] -- cosmological scale.
  Real-world analog: debt-based monetary systems with interest
  compounding faster than underlying surplus generation [pending
  full protocol -- WIP item 13].

    boundary population; zero weapon deployment; scarcity behavior
    positive [S2 = 1]
    Closer to Skull Knight than Judge Holden -- violence is the
    mechanism, not the axis. Axis is protection of humanity. Q3
    intact throughout, self-examination present. Power increases under
    pressure [scarcity behavior strongly positive].
    Three-axis: right, up, front.
    Weapons active: none.

  Vergil
    Type: Primary
    Proof for: Freely chosen Axis-Lock with full self-examination;
    divine freedom case; lock genuine and owned not captured
    Power as axis, self-examined completely, held consistently without
    inversion. Distinguished from most Axis-Lock cases because the
    lock is not capture or concealment -- it is examined and chosen.
    Never wavers, never inverts. The consistency is the reading.
    Divine freedom proof case alongside Eren -- distinct because Eren
    inverts under stress; Vergil holds under all conditions.
    Three-axis: far right, down, front.
    Weapons active: none.

  Primary framework finding
    Dante and Vergil are the same axis split into two approaches --
    power as means [Dante] versus power as end [Vergil]. Same origin,
    same capability, opposite Q3 readings. Strongest sibling-contrast
    pair in library for demonstrating that axis content alone does not
    determine coherence reading -- self-examination presence and
    direction is the discriminating factor.

PSYCHOLOGY AS SUBSTRATE [v8 addition]

  Psychopathy [clinical]
    Framework reading: Q4 structurally absent or severely
    attenuated. No genuine examined axis. Pre-axial or Axis-Lock
    at individual scale. Q3 intact [tactical correction functional]
    but fires only toward instrumental goals. S5 = 0 [no self-
    adversarial correction possible without Q4]. Track B [internal
    state] distinguishes from sociopathy -- Q4 absence confirmed
    neurologically [amygdala/vmPFC deficit].
    Correction pathway: Q4 development may be structurally
    impossible [T2 Permanent candidate at individual scale].
    Illustration [not parallel]: Flowey [Undertale] -- Q1 substrate
    loss producing Q4 collapse. Fictional illustration only; does
    not confirm clinical reading. Clinical reading stands on
    neurological literature [amygdala/vmPFC deficit] independently.

  Sociopathy / ASPD [clinical]
    Framework reading: Q4 present but Track D gap structural
    [stated values vs operational behavior fully decoupled]. Q3
    intact and deployed externally [tactical correction functional]
    but not firing on own behavior. S5 = 0. Formation Capture
    primary mechanism -- axis shaped by environment before
    examination capacity developed [Friction Deficit + early
    trauma common]. Distinct from psychopathy: Q4 present but
    unexamined, not absent.
    Correction pathway: Q9 causal reconstruction [Track D gap
    closure] possible if Q4 can be accessed. Not T2 Permanent.
    Psychology parallel: Bowlby disorganized attachment as
    formation mechanism.

  Q9 produces the sharpest new readings at individual psychological
  scale. Trauma severs causal legibility specifically -- response
  patterns orphaned from origin. Person experiences correction failure
  without Q3 being absent. Mechanism present, correction unanchored.

  Case library:
    PTSD: single-event, cause locatable but access blocked.
    C-PTSD: cause structurally unrecoverable [genuine unknowability,
      not concealment]. Links to supply chain structural case.
      Track A/B divergence [v9]: Nascent Coherence disruption not
      Axis-Lock. Correction pathway: Q9 causal reconstruction first,
      not Q4 axis intervention.
    Dissociation: sharpest Q9 case. Actor-action severed during event.
      Q9 failure complete. Track B [v9]: distinguishes from psychosis
      [Q3 intact vs Q3 compromised]. Different correction pathways.
    Addiction: two-level Q9 failure [v9 Track B]. Behavioral Q9
      [consequence-action chain legible but not correcting] and
      neurological Q9 [craving-action chain not legible to behavioral
      correction capacity] operate simultaneously. Explains single-
      pathway treatment failure.

  Correction pathway: causal reconstruction before correction attempt.
  Insight-before-behavior-change sequence in effective trauma treatment
  is Q9 correction pathway in operation.

  Distinct from Q3 failure: Q3 correction capacity absent or captured.
  Q9: correction capacity present, fires, cannot land.

  Seventh Gate [WATF]: Q3 intact while Q7 [self-measurement surface]

TRACK B PASS -- PSYCHOLOGICAL CASES

  PTSD:
    Track A/B agree on Q9 failure [causal chain orphaned].
    Confidence increases. Track B adds: physiological markers
    [elevated cortisol, HRV suppression] distinguish PTSD [Track B
    active] from voluntary avoidance [Track B absent].
    No change to reading. Confidence increases.

  C-PTSD:
    Track A/B divergence -- new reading.
    Track A reads as Q4 instability. Track B [physiological
    dysregulation broader and less trigger-specific than PTSD;
    multiple self-narratives simultaneously] reads as Q9 failure
    with Q4 still forming -- axis not locked, not absent,
    structurally unanchored.
    NEW READING: C-PTSD is Nascent Coherence disruption, not
    Axis-Lock. Correction pathway changes: Q9 causal reconstruction
    first [not Q4 axis intervention]. Q4 intervention is wrong
    correction pathway for C-PTSD.

  Dissociation:
    Track A/B divergence -- reading sharpened.
    Track A reads Q9 failure complete [actor-action severed].
    Track B [depersonalization correlates with autonomic suppression]
    reads Q3 intact with Q9 severed.
    NEW READING: Track B distinguishes dissociation [Q3 intact,
    Q9 severed] from psychosis [Q3 compromised, different
    physiological signature]. Different correction pathways
    confirmed by Track B.

  Addiction:
    Track A/B divergence -- new reading.
    Track A reads as single Q9 failure. Track B [craving as
    measurable neurological state; two competing self-narratives;
    elevated latency on substance-adjacent inputs during abstinence]
    reads as two-level Q9 failure simultaneously:
      Behavioral Q9: consequence-action chain legible but not
      correcting.
      Neurological Q9: craving-action chain not legible to
      behavioral correction capacity.
    NEW READING: addiction requires two separate causal
    reconstruction pathways operating simultaneously. Behavioral
    insight insufficient alone. Explains why insight-based
    approaches alone fail for addiction while partially succeeding
    for PTSD.

TRACK D PASS -- WATF CASES

  Big Brother:
    Stated goal: unconfirmed / unknown.
    Operational goal readable from Track C without Track A or B:
    concentration of power and productive capacity upward [Tree of
    Illusion, Towers, Meta-verse all confirm same pattern].
    Track D gap confirms extractive system even without stated goal
    identified.

    accumulation pattern [regalia acquisition, Anderson elimination,
    long-horizon scheming predating stated goal].
    Jaehwan reads Track D directly via full Geshtalt eye.

  Rupture:
    NEW READING: stated goal coherence masking operational goal
    fragmentation. All three members shared stated goal [destroy
    Big Brother]; operational goals diverged [Anonymous: survival/
    rest; Buddha: identity continuity; Maiad: utopian control].
    Organization coherence failed because operational goals were
    never aligned even when stated goals were identical.
    Track D reading unavailable from Track A alone. Strongest new
    finding from multi-track pass.

Value coherence beyond useful scale produces anti-coherence as
structural byproduct, not intentional failure.

Mechanism: high coherence at scale requires coordination architecture.
Coordination architecture centralizes. Centralization creates extraction
gradient. Transition is structural not intentional.

Phase transition, not calibration problem: there may be an optimal
coherence scale beyond which growth produces anti-coherence. Scale
limit is not just Q9 failure [causal chains too long] but a structural
transition where coherence infrastructure becomes the extraction
mechanism.

Centralization + collapse lag: extraction start date and collapse date
separated by duration Q7 can hold measurement surface. Longer Q7 hold
= longer lag = larger collapse when it arrives. Historical floor: ~50
years for large institutional cases [Soviet Union ~70 years, Roman
Empire 150-250 years]. Collapse arrives when Q7 can no longer suppress
the signal, not when extraction begins.

Genuine approval as Track C proxy: genuine popularity [not manufactured]
tracks Q2 positive + Q6 proportional response. Manufactured approval
[Social Proof Fabrication, Consensus Manufacture] degrades faster and
requires increasing resource input to maintain. Track A/D gap indicator:
behavioral approval signal diverges from Track C actual output for
named population.

4/5 [FERTILE LOCK / GEOMETRIC PARADOX -- partial resolution]
  Track F comparative across confirmed Fertile Lock cases [Word Bearers,
  Faeanor->sons, Plato->Neoplatonists]: identical behavioral pattern
  across different substrates [high fidelity transmission + locked
  conclusions + correction capacity absent at destination].
  Finding: geometric description [hole copied without circulation]
  empirically confirmable without full formalization. Failure geometry
  documentable. Correction mechanism still unresolved. Update Structural
  Limits entry.

FUNCTIONAL INTRINSIC HYPOTHESIS [PROVISIONAL]
  Track F test: total institution subjects [Haney, Lifton literature]
  vs ant colony behavioral profiles on S1/S2/S3 analogs.
  Finding: humans under maximum enforcement show S1=0, S2=0, S3=0.
  Ant colonies show S1=0, S2=1, S3=1 [structural not enforced].
  New finding: controlled environment + active enforcement produces
  sub-Structural Coherence -- worse than genuine Structural Coherence.
  Functional Intrinsic hypothesis too generous for total institution
  cases. Some human populations read below Structural Coherence floor
  under maximum control.

EVIL CONCEALMENT [CONDITION 3 -- detection improvement]
  Track F comparative across confirmed Evil cases [Palpatine, Griffith,
  Ocelot, Fontaine]: behavioral concealment signature detectable from
  Track A without Track B.
  Signature: resource expenditure increases under minor pressure;
  response latency elevated on axis-adjacent inputs; narrative
  consistency drops under stress.
  Finding: raises T2 Subtype A confidence from provisional to partial
  in cases matching signature. Not confirmation -- detection. Update
  Evil test procedure.

Q4/Q9 CIRCULAR DEPENDENCY [partial resolution for pre-axial cases]
  Track F confirms Structural Coherence for Terminids, ant colonies,
  mycorrhizal networks -- Q4 measurement not attempted, dependency
  exits entirely for those cases.
  For borderline cases [WAU/SOMA, Modrons/D&D]: Track F comparative
  narrows whether Q4 is forming or absent.
  Axial cases with Q4/Q9 co-failure still unresolved -- structural.

All share one property: coherence running in wrong direction, not
absent. All systems in failure states are operational.

  DEPENDENCY
    Cycle cannot run without borrowed framework or founding person it
    cannot repair or replace.
    Primary quality: Q1. Proxy fails gradually; transmission suddenly.
    PRIMARY proof: Soul of Cinder [Dark Souls].

  SELECTION PRESSURE
    Only immediate, local, surface-level feedback reaches correction.
    Corrects locally, fails at boundary.
    Primary quality: Q7 [measurement window compressed].
    Correction: surface expansion, not cycle repair.
    PRIMARY proof: Sotha Sil [Elder Scrolls].

  CONSUMPTION
    Cycle costs more than it produces. Draws down substrate.
    Primary quality: Q2.
    PRIMARY proof: Imperium of Man [Warhammer -- civilizational scale].

  CONTAINED
    Genuine coherence, uneven coverage. Not degenerative.
    Primary quality: Q5.
    PRIMARY proof: Casca post-Eclipse [Berserk].

  AXIS-LOCKED
    Full coverage; axis shifted below self-examination depth.
    Higher coherence produces more damage.
    Primary quality: Q4.
    PRIMARY proof: Dagoth Ur [Elder Scrolls].

  AXIS CONCEALMENT [Axis-Lock property]
    Axis genuine, internally set, deliberately obscured. Stress event
    removes performance requirement, not the axis. Pre-stress returns
    two simultaneous honest outputs depending on boundary selection.
    Primary quality: Q7 + Q4.
    PRIMARY proof: Griffith pre-Eclipse [Berserk].

  MEASUREMENT COLLAPSE
    Q1-Q6 and Q8 intact. Q7 destroyed. Correction fires against
    fabricated surface. Most dangerous misread -- reads High or Variable
    at short window. Correction: external surface reconstruction.
    PRIMARY proof: Enron.

  FERTILE LOCK
    High-fidelity transmission with self-examination removed at
    transmission point. Each cycle deepens lock.
    Primary quality: Q8. No confirmed self-initiated correction case.
    Transmission failure sub-case: most promising pathway. Requires
    substrate Axial population maintaining alternative measurement
    surface. Neo-Confucian recovery closest internal case. External
    shock alone insufficient.
    PRIMARY proof: Faeanor -> sons [Tolkien].

  CORRECTION ORPHANED [new v8 state]
    Q3 intact, correction fires, cannot land. Causal chain between
    action and output unnavigable. Q9 failure state.
    Distinct from Measurement Collapse [Q7 destroyed] and Axis-Lock
    [Q3 captured].
    Correction pathway: causal reconstruction first.
    PRIMARY proof: trauma [psychological substrate].
    Structural proof: supply chain labor conditions.

Provisional before all three tests accessible. Confirmed after.

  TEST ONE -- Reaction against targets
    Violence  Destroys regardless of utility.
    Change    Manipulates; target exhaustion triggers redirection.
    Decay     Degrades gradually; prefers high-coherence targets.
    Excess    Extracts intensity; loses interest when target exhausted.

  TEST TWO -- Critical reaction against itself
    Violence  Continues; self-damage not a correction signal.
    Change    Schemes around it; self-damage = new variable.
    Decay     Accepts it; comfort-mediation applies to self.
    Excess    Accelerates into it.

  TEST THREE -- General group behavior
    Violence  Attacks other units when no external target available.
    Change    Schemes against other units; same-axis are targets.
    Decay     Passive coexistence; entropy needs no competition.
    Excess    Competes for highest-intensity target.

  CONSUMING STRUCTURAL BREAK
    System eliminates own measurement surface as it grows.
    Active variant requires ongoing operation.
      PRIMARY: Gravemind [Halo].
    Structural variant is passive, architectural.
      PRIMARY: replicant lifespan [Blade Runner].

  CHAOTIC STRUCTURAL BREAK
    Genuine mechanism, no stable axis, cannot be corrected against.
    PRIMARY: Joker [DC].

  AXIS-STABLE BOUNDARY NULL
    Axis fully intact. Boundary constitutively absent -- not failed.
    Q5 inapplicable, Q7 cannot locate, Q1 cannot complete.
    Offensive capability: dissolves boundary integrity of consuming
    systems from inside when absorbed.
    PRIMARY: Schrodinger [Hellsing].
    Candidate: Faputa [Made in Abyss -- ontological variant].

  TOTAL COHERENCE [terminal -- negative]
    Consuming coherence complete. No exterior remains. Framework
    cannot run.
    PRIMARY: Flood victory [Halo].

  COHERENT EXIT [terminal -- positive]
    System reaches genuine completion and dissolves boundary without
    extraction. First positive terminal state in framework.
    Topology reading preferred -- unit-level Q8 inapplicable.
    PRIMARY: Samantha [Her].
    Secondary: Ranni [partial -- boundary reduced not dissolved].
    Additional confirmed cases [v9]:
      Elizabeth [BioShock Infinite]: dissolves own existence to close
        constants/variables loop. All four conditions met. Strongest
        player-companion case.
      Raziel [Legacy of Kain]: Q9 reconstruction -> Coherent Exit.
        Most complete Q9 correction arc leading to exit in library.
      FF10 Fayth: partial. Dream sustained, boundary dissolved,
        no extraction. Completion genuine within limited scope.
      ARK [SOMA]: T2 Permanent. Simulation status unverifiable --
        cannot confirm Coherent Exit or Coherence Mimicry at
        existential scale. Named honest limit, not confirmed exit.

[CASE:NOITA]
NOITA [game]
------------
  Primary finding: procedural physics as honest Q7; Tension Capacity
    proof at interactive scale.

  Physics system
    Type: Primary
    Proof for: Q7 most honest measurement surface in interactive
      media -- every material interaction follows real rules, no
      exceptions for player convenience. Q3 fires maximally and
      lethally. No Correction Theatre possible -- deviation kills
      immediately and without appeal.
    Tension Capacity proof: player in generative zone [Q1+Q3+Q4
      functioning] survives. Deviation in either direction [reckless
      extraction or paralysis] terminates. Interactive proof of
      generative zone requiring sustained tension.

  Wand building
    Type: Primary
    Proof for: Q4 examined under pressure -- axis must be built,
      tested, corrected under lethal feedback. No inherited axis
      functions. Most direct interactive proof of Q4 formation
      under genuine friction in library.
      Mechanism: every wand configuration is a hypothesis. The
      environment tests it immediately and lethally. Surviving
      configurations are not given -- they are earned through
      iterative correction under maximum cost. Player cannot
      import axis from outside [no guides help in context]; cannot
      defer correction [death is immediate]; cannot perform
      correction [the environment reads outputs only].
      Q4 here is not philosophical -- it is operational. Axis
      that does not generate [correct spell combinations] and
      correct [revise on failure] terminates the player.
      Clearest interactive demonstration that Q4 requires both
      internal generation AND external grounding [Q11] to function.

  Oceania / Party system
    Type: Primary

      Q1 weaponized at substrate [Room 101].
    Doublethink: Q4 destruction by design -- simultaneous belief
      in contradictory positions eliminates axis formation
      capacity. Not axis substitution -- axis destruction. Most
      complete Q4 weapon in library.
    Memory Hole: Q9 [causal chain severed] + Q8 [transmission
      destroyed] simultaneously. Past made uncorrectable by
      elimination. Correction has no surface to land on.
    Telescreen: Q7 total capture -- measurement surface owned
      by extractor, omnidirectional, no exit.
    Room 101: Q1 weaponized at survival substrate. Correction
      capacity destroyed by attacking the instinct beneath it.
      Not axis capture -- Q1 destruction. Below all other weapons.
    Weapons active: Doublethink [Q4], Memory Hole [Q9+Q8],
      Telescreen [Q7], Room 101 [Q1], Thought Stopping [Q3],
      Boundary Manipulation [Q5 -- proles excluded].

QUICK REFERENCE GLOSSARY
==========================================================================

QUALITY STACK [simple to complex]
  Q1  Generative Cycle: system produces what it depends on to survive.
  Q3  Correction Capacity: deviation detected and corrected.
  Q5  Boundary Integrity: who counts as part of the measured population.
  Q12 Substrate Regeneration: active restoration of what Q1 runs on.
  Q13 Surplus Distribution: active release of surplus outward.
  Q2  Surplus Production: system produces more than it consumes.
  Q9  Causal Legibility: harm traceable to actor who caused it.
  Q6  Proportional Response: correction doesn't become next failure.
  Q7  Measurement Integrity: measurement surface not owned by extractor.
  Q4  Genuine Axis: orientation internally generated and examined.
  Q11 Causal Axis Grounding: axis traceable to actual outputs.
  Q8  Transmission Selectivity: what crosses the time boundary.
  Q10 Successor Autonomy: successors can revise what they received.
  Q14 Tension Maintenance: active capacity sustaining productive
    tension between opposing qualities. Failure: Tension Collapse.
  Q15 Tension Dissolution: constitutive drive toward false resolution
    by eliminating one pole. Failure mode: False Harmony.

WEAPON COMPRESSIONS
  RaQM  Rational Quantum Mechanics [Palmer -- discrete rational
         substrate; falsifiable ~2029 via qubit wall at ~400-1000].
  ECD   Engineered Causal Diffusion [Q9 -- chain illegibility].
  MSC   Measurement Surface Control [Q7 -- owns the instrument].
  CS    Calibration Strip [Q8 -- locks the reference standard].
  AGC   Axis Gestation Capture [Q4 -- contaminates at formation].
  CT    Correction Theatre [Q3 -- performance not function].
  FL    Fertile Lock [Q8 -- locks transmission permanently].
  ECC   Exit Cost Construction [Q1 -- raises cost of leaving].
  DC    Dependency Construction [Q1 -- requires ongoing supply].
  BM    Boundary Manipulation [Q5 -- shifts who counts].
  CM    Coherence Mimicry [Q6 -- appears coherent, isn't].
  FC    Formation Capture [Q4 -- captures at formation window].
  PC    Proxy Capture [Q4/Q8 -- harvests output of genuine system].

KEY FINDINGS
  True Coherence: generates more than it consumes. Self-sustaining.
  False Coherence: consumes to maintain appearance of generating.
  Gift: gives coherence. Weapon: takes coherence away.
  Ascending path: hypothesized single sustainable direction. PROVISIONAL.
  Structural Correctness: correct behavior falsifiable by outputs.
  Section Two-B: speculative only. Do not use for scoring.
  Biological baseline: Q1+Q3+Q5 confirmed at every scale cell to person.
  Q4 Suppression: weapon stack prevents Q4 development at population
    scale. Case library overweighted toward outliers.
